MADRAS.





mukavuri



acōkaṉatu cāsaṉaṅkaḷ ulaka carittirattilēyē oru putumi eṉalām. āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kalveṭṭukkaḷum vēṟu palavita purātaṉa likitaṅkaḷum namakku ippuviyiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷiliruntu kiṭittirukkiṉṟaṉa ; āyiṉum taṉ pirajikaḷiṉ kṣēmattiṉ poruṭṭu, tarmōpatēcaṅkaḷi eḻutivitta aracaṉi nām vēṟeṅkuṅ kaṇṭilēm. inta likitaṅkaḷ intiya carittirattil mikavum ēṟṟamuṭiya tastāvējukaḷ, mutalāvatu, ivaṟṟiviṭap paḻimiyāṉa likitaṅkaḷ anēkamāka intiyāviṟ kiṭiyā. ivaṟṟiṉ karutti aṟiyum poruṭṭuc ceyyappaṭṭa ārāyccikaḷ intiyāviṟ cilācāsaṉa ārāyccikkut toṭakkamākum. iraṇṭāvatu, ivaṟṟiṟ kāṇappaṭum lipi (eḻuttu) nam nāṭṭil taṟkālam upayōkap paṭuttappaṭum pala eḻuttukkaḷiṉ ātiyi viḷakkukiṟatu. mūṉṟāvatu, putta mata carittiratti viḷakka iccāsaṉaṅkaḷ iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa, nāṉkāvatu, inta likitaṅkaḷiluḷḷa pāṣi ki. mu. mūṉṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭintiyāvil pēcappaṭṭu vanta moḻiyāṉatu vaṭamoḻiyōṭum pirākruta naṭikaḷōṭum evvita oṟṟumi vēṟṟumikaḷi uṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa eṉpati viḷakkum. ilakkiyamākak karutumiṭattum acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷ mikac cirēṣṭamāṉavikaḷē. ivaṟṟiṟ peruntaṉmiyuṭiya ōr aracaṉ itayattik kāṇkiṟōm ; avaṉuṭiya uḷḷattiṉ uyarvum kaṉivum evariyum viyappaṭiyac ceyyumeṉpatiṟ cantēkamilli.

icciṟu pustakatti vācikkum aṉpar yāvarum intiya carittirattippaṟṟiya ārāyccikaḷiṟ piriyam koḷḷat tūṇṭappaṭalām. ēṉeṉṟāl, carittirattiṉ mūlātāramāyuḷḷa tastāvējukaḷit tāmē paṭittu viṣayaṅkaḷi āyvatu mikavum iṉpamāṉa kāriyamāyirukkavēṇṭum. evvaḷavu virivāka eḻutappaṭṭirukkum carittirap pustakaṅkaḷip paṭippatum itaṟku nikarākātu.



ippustakattil avatāriki caṟṟu virivāka amintiruppatu oru kuṟiyākāteṉṟu karutukiṟēṉ. acōkaṉ cariti, āṭcimuṟi, akkālattup perumiyit terivikkum aṟikuṟikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi vivarippatu iccāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ mukkiyatti uṇaruvataṟku avaciyamāṉatāl avatārikiyil ivviṣayaṅkaḷ viḷakkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.

cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppu, iṅkilīṣ peyarppukkaḷi maṭṭum aṉusarittirāmal mūlattōṭum oppiṭṭuc cīrtiruttappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. palavita iyaṟki vittiyācaṅkaḷ niṟintiruntapōtum intiyāvil nuṭpamāṉa oṟṟumi uḷḷūṟap paraviyiruppatupōla, intiya moḻikaḷukkum neruṅkiya uṟavu. irukkiṉṟatu. utāraṇamāka, samskirutam pirākirutam mūlamāka vanta coṟkaḷ pala pāṣikaḷukkum potuvāka uḷḷaṉa; iḵtaṉṟi, vācakarītiyilum aṇiyilum oṟṟumi kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ātiyil innāṭṭup pāṣiyoṉṟil eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkiyattiṟku maṟṟoru intiyamoḻiyiṟ ceyyappaṭum peyarppu iṅkilīṣ moḻipeyarppiviṭa mēṉmiyāyiruppatu iyalpaṉṟō? ākiyāl acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ tamiḻ moḻi peyarppu iṅkilīṣ moḻipeyarppiviṭa mūlattiṉ pōkki viḷakka ēṟṟatāyirukkavēṇṭum. appaṭiyillāviṭṭāl aḵtu āciriyaṉ kuṟṟamēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu; karuttu viḷaṅkuvataṟkuc ciṟitum kaṣṭamiṉṟiyum cilacamayam taṉṉi aṟiyāmalum mūlattiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ it tamiḻuriyil vantiruppatu ivvapippirāyattukkuc cāṉṟākum.

ivvita pirayattaṉaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭitta utavikaḷik kūṟuvatu marapu; iḵtu eṉatu piriyamāṉa kaṭamiyākavumirukkiṉṟatu. ceṉṉi carvakalācāliyiṉ intiya carittira āciriyarākiya ṭākṭar es. kiruṣṇasvāmi aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ puttakattiṉ eḻuttup piratiyip paricōtittuc cila kuṟṟaṅkuṟikaḷit tīrttuḷḷārkaḷ. tiruvaṉantapuram kiranta paripālaṉālayattic cērnta ma-⁰⁰-⁰⁰-srī hariharacāstirikaḷ eṉakku mutalil im muyaṟcikku ūkkattik koṭuttār. maṟṟum pala naṇparkaḷ itaṉ eḻuttuppiratiyiyō allatu accup piratiyiyō vācittup pala kuṟikaḷit tīrttirukkiṉṟaṉar. iṅkilīṣil acōkaṉippaṟṟiya viyācaṅkaḷum, pustakaṅkaḷum, vicēṣamāka srī. viṉseṇṭ smittiṉ "acōkaṉ" eṉṟa pustakamum srī pūlariṉ cāsaṉa moḻipeyarppum eṉakku mikunta upayōkamāyiruntaṉa. intiyaṉ āṉṭikkuvari mutaliya ārāyccip pattirikikaḷil acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷil varum coṟkaḷ, coṟṟoṭarkaḷ, vākkiyaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ karuttip paṟṟip pala vātaṅkaḷ vantuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiyum nāṉ iyaṉṟavāṟu upayōkappaṭutti yirukkiṟēṉ.

70-m pakkattiluḷḷa cittiramum 150-m pakkattiluḷḷa kalveṭṭum "eppikrāpiyā intikkā" VIII, V m vālyuṅkaḷiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭavi.

cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ mūlattiyum tēvanākara eḻuttilō kiranta eḻuttilō eḻuti ittuṭaṉ piracurañ ceyyavēṇṭumeṉṟa eṉ viruppam virivil kikūṭumeṉṟu niṉikkiṟēṉ. moḻi peyarppi mūlattōṭu oppiṭac cukariyappaṭumāṟu ovvoru cāsaṉamum mūlattil ittaṉi vākkiyaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu ataṉ kīḻuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷiṟ kāṭṭap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa, acōka likitaṅkaḷ 'eppikrāpiyā intikkā' I, II, III, V, VIII vālyuṅkaḷilum intiyaṉ āṉṭikkuvari. XIX, XX, XXII vālyuṅkaḷilum āṅkila eḻuttiṟ piracuramāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. kalkattā yūṉivarviṭṭiyiṉ patippu iccāsaṉaṅkaḷi kikku aṭakkamāka oru ciṟu puttakamākkiyirukkiṟatu. ivaṟṟil āṅkila eḻuttil eḻutip piracurañ ceyyappaṭṭirukkum mūlatti ittamiḻ moḻipeyarppuṭaṉum acciṭa uttēcikkiṉṟēṉ.





poruḷaṭakkam





* * *



avatāriki



I intiyāviṉ pūrvacarittira ārāycci

carittira aṟiviṉ vaḷarcci. pūrva intiya carittirattiṉ ātāraṅkaḷ. acōkaṉatu cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ karutti viḷakkiya ārāyccikaḷ. ... 1—8

II. acōkaṉ cariti:-

makatatēcattiṉ perumi. pintucāraṉ. acōkaṉip paṟṟi aitihyaṅkaḷiliruntu kiṭikkum vivaraṅkaḷ. aracaṉ paṭṭāpiṣēkam, kaliṅkayuttam. puttamatattiṉ sparcam. tīrtta yāttirikaḷ. tuṟavi aracu naṭattiya vinti. tarmappiracāram ceytal. veku tūrattuḷḷa aintu aracaruṭaṉ uṟavu. itiliruntu kiṭikkum "sama kālattuvam." kuṇatici ulakam kuṭatici ulakattic cantittatu. ājīvakarkaḷukkuk kukikaḷ ceytal. stampacāsaṉaṅkaḷip piracurañ ceytal. putta makāsapi. acōkaṉ kuṇam. acōkaṉukkuppiṉ vanta mōriya aracar. ... 8—30

III. acōkaṉ tarmam:—

acōkaṉ kālattil putta matattil ēṟpaṭṭa māṟutalkaḷ. putta caṅka capikaḷ. mūṉṟāvatu caṅka capi. saṉmārkka pōtaṉiyē putta matattiṉ sāram eṉka. mūṭa nampikkikaḷik kaṇṭittal. jīva imsi nivāraṇam. cuvarkkattil nampikki. acōkaṉukkup piṉ putta matattil ēṟpaṭṭa māṟutalkaḷ. ... 31-43

IV. acōkaṉ aracāṭci:—

acōkaṉ turittaṉam muṉṉiruntataṉ toṭarcciyē. aracaṉatu putu nōkkaṅkaḷ. camarasapāvam. kṣēmāpivirttikkāṉa putuēṟpāṭukaḷ. vivacāyam, kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ, cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum atikārikaḷ. aṉusamyāṉam. acōka ēkātipattiyattiṉ virivu. ...... 43-58

V. akkālattup paḻamporuḷkaḷ:—

ivaṟṟilum paḻamiyāṉavi intiyāvil kiṭiyā. acōkaṉuṭiya vēlikaḷ. ivaṟṟiṉ taṟkāla aṟikuṟikaḷ. stūpaṅkaḷ. sāñcistūpam. parkhūt stūpam, kayi stūpam, stūpaṅkaḷil uṇṭāṉa māṟutalkaḷ. kukikaḷ. stampaṅkaḷ. ivaṟṟiṉ ciṟpattiṟami. intac ciṟpattip paṟṟiya matippu. .... 58-69

VI. acōka eḻuttu:—

iruvita purātaṉa lipikaḷ. pirāmmi lipi intiya eḻuttukkaḷukku mūlātāram. intiya pāṣikaḷukku lipi ēṟpaṭṭa vitam. karōṣṭi lipi. ... 69-73

VII. piriyatarci aracaṉ kalveṭṭukkaḷ:—

ivaṟṟiṉ pākupāṭu. ivaṟṟiṉ taṉmi. vācakarīti. cācaṉaṅkaḷ allāta likitaṅkaḷ. likitaṅkaḷiṉ pirivu. acōka likitaṅkaḷiṉ pāṣi. ilakkaṇak kuṟippu. .... 73-80

acōkaṉuṭiya cāsaṉaṅkaḷ



avatāriki

1. intiyāviṉ pūrva carittira ārāycci



intiyāviṉ pūrva carittiramāṉatu māṉiṭa jātiyiṉ muṉṉēṟṟatti viḷakkuvataṟku mika ariya cātaṉamām. intiya carittirattiluḷḷa araca parampariyil acōkaṉ ōr naṭunāyakamāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟāṉ. ivaṉuṭiya viṉōtamāṉa cāsaṉaṅkaḷip paṭittuṇara ivvaracaṉippaṟṟiya ellā viṣayaṅkaḷiyum muṉṉuriyākak kūṟuvatu avaciyameṉak karutukiṟōm.

nūṟṟimpatu varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉvariyum mēṉāṭukaḷil intiyāviṉ pūrvika carittirattippaṟṟi mikak kuṟivāṉa aṟivutāṉ iruntatu. appōtu mukammatiyar varukikku muṉṉuḷḷa kālattiṉ carittiratti yārum terintu koḷḷavilli, ki. pi. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṭivil, mēl nāṭṭār samskiruta ilakkiyam eṉṟa makācamuttirattiṉ āḻattiyum viriviyum uṇara ārampittaṉar. āyiṉum, ulakattiṉ carittirattil intiya carittiramāṉatu mikavum mukkiyamāṉa pākameṉpatu oruvarukkum pulappaṭavilli. nam nāṭṭu vittuvāṉkaḷō, carittiram eṉṟa viṣayatti āḻnta paṭippum ārāycciyum avaciyamāṉa oru cās tiramākak karutavilli. mēṉāṭṭārō, tamatu pirakiruti cāstirat tērcciyilum nākarikattiṉ mēṉmiyilum tāmē mayaṅkit taṅkaḷukkup puviyil evarum nikarilli yeṉṟum maṟṟa jātiyāriṉ carittiram ulakattiṉ muṟpōkkukku avaciyamilliyeṉṟum niṉittu iṟu māppaṭintiruntaṉar. ōr putu tiṉam uṇṭāvatu pōla meḷḷa meḷḷa iruḷ akaṉṟu ippōtu oru putu uṇarcci paravivarukiṟatu, inta nūṟu varuṣaṅkaḷukkuk kiṭiyil intiyāviṉ carittiram ulakattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattukku evvaḷavu avaciyamāṉatu eṉpati aṟiñar uṇarntu varukiṉṟaṉar.

pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil intiyāviṉ ﻿

carittira aṟiviṉ

vaḷarcci

﻿nākarikattip paṟṟiya ārāycci toṭaṅkiṟṟu. ataṉ piṟakum pala varuṣaṅkaḷukku nam tēcattiṉ pūrvacarittiram uḷḷē piravēcikka vaḻiyillāta peruṅ kāṭāyiruntatu. virivāṉa purāṇak katikaḷum tarma cāstiraṅkaḷum tattuva vētāntak kirantaṅkaḷum aitihyaṅkaḷu mallāmal, kālanirṇayamuḷḷa vivaraṅkaḷ ic carittirattil kāṇappaṭavilli. toṭakkam, muṭivu, elli mutaliya yātoru aḷavumillāmalum kāla variyaṟi yillāmalum tokukkappaṭṭa pustakaṅkaḷ intiyāviṉ carittiramāy viḷaṅkiṉa. ippaṭippaṭṭa kāṭṭiṉūṭē nuḻintu cella namatu "purātaṉavastu cāstirikaḷ” ōr vaḻiyi amittirukkiṟārkaḷ.



purātaṉa vastu cāstiram eṉṟāl eṉṉa? paṇṭikkāla nilimiyiṉ aṟikuṟiyāka ittēcam muḻumiyilum parantu kiṭakkum iṭinta kōyilkaḷ, pāḻāṉa nakaraṅkaḷ, iṉṉum citiyāta maṟṟa cātaṉaṅkaḷ, putiyalāy namakku akappaṭum paṟpalavita nāṇayaṅkaḷ, ceppuppaṭṭayaṅkaḷ, kalveṭṭukaḷ, stampaṅkaḷ, piratimikaḷ mutaliya vastukkaḷi “purātaṉa vastu”[1] eṉṟu nām iṅku kūṟuvōm. ivaṟṟiliruntu nam ārāycciyiṉ payaṉāka namakkuk kiṭittirukkum aṟivē purātaṉa vastu cāstiram[2].

pūrva intiya

carittirattiṉ

ātāraṅkaḷpūrvika intiya carittirattiṉ ātāraṅkaḷi mūṉṟu vakiyākap pirikkalām.

mutalāvatu, aitihyam. atāvatu intiyāviluḷḷa ellāvita kirantaṅkaḷilum aṭaṅkiya vivaraṅkaḷ. curuti, smiruti, vētāṅkaṅkaḷ, itikāsam, purāṇam, eṉṟa palavaki kirantaṅkaḷiyum ippirivil nām aṭakkukiṟōm. intak kirantaṅkaḷippōlavē ivaṟṟil aṭaṅkiya camācāraṅkaḷum aḷavaṟṟavi.

iraṇṭāvatu, anniya nāṭṭār intiyāvippaṟṟit tam tam pāṣikaḷil eḻuti vittuḷḷa vivaraṅkaḷ alaksāntaruṭaṉum ataṟkup piṉṉum vanta yavaṉa tūtarkaḷum yavaṉa tēcāntarikaḷum pāhiyaṉ, yuvaṉcuvaṅ, itsiṅ mutaliya cīṉa tēcāntarikaḷum, tāṅkaḷ it tēcattil kaṇṭatum kēṭṭatumāṉa ceytikaḷi eḻutivittirukkiṟārkaḷ. avi yāvum akappaṭavilliyāyiṉum kiṭittavariyilum iv vātāraṅkaḷ kāla nirṇayattukku mika mukkiyamāṉavi. mūṉṟāvatu, purātaṉa vastucāstiram. itil mūṉṟu mukkiyamāṉa uṭpirivukaḷ uṇṭu. (i) paḻiya kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷic cōtaṉi ceyvatiliruntum ciṟpaṅkaḷil kālattukkut takkapaṭi ēṟpaṭum vittiyācaṅkaḷik kavaṉippatiliruntum uṇṭākum aṟivu. (Architecture and Sculpture). (ii) pāṟiyilum kōyilkaḷiṉ kaṟcuvarilum ceppup paṭṭayaṅkaḷilum varintu kāṇappaṭum vikitaṅkaḷip paṭippataṉāl kiṭikkum aṟivu. (Epigraphy). (iii) paḻiyakālattu nāṇayaṅkaḷip paricōtaṉi ceyvataṉ payaṉāka uṇṭākum aṟivu (Numismatics). mutalāvatu pirivic cērnta aitihyam eṉṟa ātārattiliruntu namakkuk kiṭittuḷḷa vivaraṅkaḷ aḷavaṟṟaviyāṉālum avaṟṟi oḻuṅku paṇṇik kālattiṉpaṭi varicippaṭuttiyatu, purātaṉa vastu ārāycciyiṉ utaviyum anniya nāṭṭār eḻuti vittuḷḷa vivaraṇaṅkaḷiṉ utaviyumē. itaṉṟi ikkāriyam acāttiyamāyiruntirukkum. purātaṉa vastu cāstirattiṉ utavi pūrvika intiyāviṉ carittirattukku evvaḷavu mukkiya ātāramākumeṉṟu uṇara acōkaṉatu cāsaṉaṅkaḷ takka cāṉṟākum.

acōkaṉ eṉṟa peyaruṭiya ōr aracaṉ intiyāvil ﻿

acōkaṉatu

cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ

karutti viḷakkiya

ārāyccikaḷ

﻿āṇṭatāka intukkaḷukku nūṟu varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ variyum koñcamum teriyāmaliruntatu. ēṟakkuṟiya āyira varuṣaṅkaḷāka avaṉ peyarē intiyariṉ ñāpakattiliruntu māyntu pōyiruntatu. vaṭa intiyāviṉ pala pākaṅkaḷil niṉṟirunta acōka stampaṅkaḷi muṉ kālattilirunta rākṣacariṉ vēliyeṉṟu nam jaṉaṅkaḷ kūṟivantaṉar. stampaṅkaḷilum pāṟikaḷilum eḻutappaṭṭirunta likitaṅkaḷukku oruvarukkum poruḷ teriyavilli. ippaṭi irukkiyil 1834ām ௵l īsṭu intiyāk 'kampaṉiyāriṉ nāṇayap paricōtaṉi atikāriyākiya jēms priṉsep eṉpavarukku apūrvamāṉa paḻiya nāṇayaṅkaḷ pala kiṭittaṉa. inta nāṇayaṅkaḷiṉ oru pakkattil krīk lipiyilum maṟṟap pakkattil appōtu intiyāvil vaḻaṅkivanta iruvaki lipi oṉṟilum vākkiyaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ippaṭippaṭṭa nāṇayaṅkaḷi tvipāṣi nāṇayaṅkaḷ[3] eṉṟu nām collalām. inta nāṇayaṅkaḷ intiyāviṉ vaṭamēṟkup pākattil ki. mu. 200 mutal ki. pi. 100 variyum āṇṭuvanta krīk allatu yavaṉa aracarkaḷāl aṭikkappaṭṭavi. krīk pāṣiyilum krīk lipiyilum inta nāṇayaṅkaḷic ceyta aracarkaḷiṉ peyarum virutukaḷum ivaṟṟiṉ oru pakkattil eḻutappaṭṭiruntaṉa. airōppiya vittuvāṉ' āṉatiṉāl jēms priṉseppukku krīkkil eḻutappaṭṭirukkum vivaraṅkaḷi vācittu aṟintukoḷvatu eḷitāyiruntatu. nāṇayaṅkaḷiṉ maṟupakkattil eḻutiyiruppatu krīkkil eḻutappaṭṭa vivaraṅkaḷēyeṉṟu avar ūkittār. aṟiyappaṭāta iruvaki lipikaḷiṉ ovvoru eḻuttiyum maṟupakkattiluḷḷa krīk lipiyōṭu ottuppārttu avveḻuttukkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉa olikaḷik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭum paṭṭiki oṉṟi priṉsep tayārceytār. stampaṅkaḷilum pāṟikaḷilum veṭṭappaṭṭirunta likitaṅkaḷil tvipāṣi nāṇayaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum intiya lipikaḷē upayōkap paṭuttappaṭṭiruppatu piṉpu teriyavantatu. paḻiya kalveṭṭukkaḷi vācittu aṟintukoḷḷap piriṉceppiṉ paṭṭiki mikunta pirayōjaṉamāyiṟṟu. āyiṉum likitaṅkaḷiṉ karuttu viḷaṅkuvatu atikak kaṣṭamāyiruntatu.

acōkaṉatu cāsaṉaṅkaḷippaṭikka muyaṉṟavaril mutalvarum inta priṉsep tāṉ. āṅkilamoḻiyil piracittipeṟṟa “rājaputtira carittiram” eḻutiya karṉal ṭāṭ (Colonel Tod) ivarip piṉpaṟṟi kirnār eṉṟa iṭattiluḷḷa likitaṅkaḷippaṭikka muyaṉṟār. mutalil inta likitaṅkaḷiṉ karuttu oruvarukkum teṉpaṭavilli. piyatasi eṉṟa ōr aracaṉāl eḻutappaṭṭa likitaṅkaḷ ivi eṉṟu maṭṭum teriyavantatu. āṉāl pala camucayaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. cila likitaṅkaḷil, tēvāṉām - priyaṉ (= tēvarkaḷukkup piriyamāṉavaṉ) eṉṟu aracaṉiṉ virutu peyarāka upayōkikkappaṭṭiruntatu. maṟṟa likitaṅkaḷil piyatasi eṉṟa peyarmaṭṭuntāṉ kāṇappaṭṭatu. vēṟu likitaṅkaḷil piyatasi eṉṟa peyarukku ‘tēvāṉāmpiriyaṉ’ eṉṟa patam virutāka upayōkappaṭṭiruntatu. iv vivaraṅkaḷiliruntu pala kēḷvikaḷ piṟantaṉa: intap piyatasi evar? piyatasi oruvarā palarā? piyatasi eṉṟa peyar ciṟappuppeyarā? allatu potuppeyarā? piyatasiyum tēvāṉāmpiriyamṉum vevvēṟu maṉitarkaḷā, allatu iraṇṭum orē maṉitaṉiṉ peyarā? ippaṭippaṭṭa kēḷvikaḷukkuk cila varuṣaṅkaḷāka viṭi kiṭikkavilli.

piṟaku purātaṉavastu cāstirikaḷum maṟṟuḷḷa vāṉkaḷum pala tuṟikaḷil ārāyccivēliyi toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. airōppiyaruḷ srī. kaṉṉiṅhām (Cunningham) parjes, (Bargess) pūlar (Bihler) seṉārṭ, (Senart) porṉāp (Burnouf) eṉpōrum, nammavaruḷ srī. rāmakiruṣṇa pantarkkar, pakavaṉlāl intirājī, rājēntiralāl mittirar, eṉṟa vitvāṉkaḷum acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ karutti viḷakkuvataṟkup pirayācappaṭṭārkaḷ. paḻiya lipikaḷ ellām vācikkappaṭṭaṉa ; purātaṉa vastukkaḷ cōtaṉi ceyyappaṭṭuk kāppāṟṟappaṭṭaṉa; likitaṅkaḷ cariyāṉa nakalkaḷuṭaṉum moḻipeyarppukaḷuṭaṉum viyākkiyāṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉum piracurañ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa ; hintu, jeyiṉa putta mataṅkaḷiccārnta kirantaṅkaḷ vivēcaṉa puttiyuṭaṉ paṭikkappaṭṭamiyāl avaṟṟiliruntu pūrvika carittirattukkup pala ātāraṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa ; acōkaṉ eṉṟa peyaruṭiya aracaṉ oru kālattil intiyā muḻuvatiyum āṇṭu vantavaṉeṉṟum avaṉukkup piyatasi eṉṟa virutu iruntateṉṟum puttak kirantaṅkaḷiliruntu teriyavantatu. puttarkaḷatu teyva pāṣiyākiya pālikkum acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ pāṣikkumuḷḷa neruṅkiya campantam veḷiyāyiṟṟu. likitaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭirukkum piyatasi, tēvāṉām piriyaṉ, tēvāṉāmpiriyaṉāṉa piyatasi, ellām orē maṉitaṉik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, peyaril eṉṉa māṟupā ṭiruntapōtilum inta likitaṅkaḷ vācakarītiyil orē maṉitaṉiṉ vākkiyaṅkaḷeṉṟu niccayamāyc collakkūṭiya oṟṟumiyiyuṭiyaṉa veṉṟum, ellōrum aṟiyalāyiṉar. kaṭiciyāka, ki. pi. 1915m ௵l reyccūrukku arukiluḷḷa māski eṉṟa ūril kiṭitta acōkak kalveṭṭil acōkaṉ eṉṟa peyarē varintiruppatu teriyavantatum inta likitaṅkaḷi eḻutiyavaṉ evaṉ eṉṟa caṅki muṟṟilum nīṅki viṭṭatu.



↑ † Antiquities purātaṉa vastukkaḷ.

↑ ★ Archeology

↑ Bi-lingual coins





kuṟippukaḷ





II. acōkaṉiṉ cariti



ki. mu. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭil makatatēcam vaṭa ﻿

makatatēcattiṉ

perumi.

﻿intiyāviluḷḷa tēcaṅkaḷil mutaṉmiyi aṭintiruntatu. in nāṭṭi āṇṭuvanta nantar eṉṟa aracarkaḷ iḻivu kulattōrākak karutappaṭṭa pōtilum parākramacālikaḷākavum yuttaciṉṉiyaṅkaḷilum taḷavāṭaṅkaḷilum valimi mikuntavarākavumiruntaṉar. akanāṉūṟu eṉṟa paṇṭit tamiḻ nūliluḷḷa oru pāṭṭil iv vamicattiṉar “palpukaḻ niṟinta velpōr nantar” eṉṟu ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭṭiruttali iṅku kuṟippiṭuvatu takutiyākum. inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil, atāvatu cumār ki mu. 325m ௵l, makatanāṭṭil oru puraṭci ēṟpaṭṭatu. cāṇakkiyaṉ, viṣṇukuptaṉ, kuṭalyaṉ, kuṭilyaṉ eṉṟa peyarkaḷiyuṭiya ōr pirāmaṇa mantiriyiṉ cūḻccikaḷ kāraṇamāka nantar aracurimiyi iḻakkavum cantirakuptaṉ eṉṟa muriyavamcattiṉaṉ aracaṉākavum nērntaṉa. cantirakuptaṉ ellā vaṭa intiya rājyaṅkaḷiyum kippaṟṟi yāvaṟṟiyum orē kuṭiyiṉ kīḻk koṇṭuvantāṉ. ataṟku muṉ ittakiya vicālamāṉa ēkātipattiyam intiyāvil ēṟpaṭavilliyeṉṟu nām collalām. cantirakuptaṉiṉ varalāṟṟi viritturippatu nam viṣayattukkup puṟamāyiṉum kuṭalyaṉāl eḻutappaṭṭa ‘arttacāstiram’ eṉṟa nūlip paṟṟi iv vavatārikiyiṉ maṟṟoru pākattil kūṟuvatu avaciyam. inta nūl ikkālattukku muṉṉum piṉṉumuḷḷa turittaṉattiṉ amippiyum rājīya nōkkaṅkaḷiyum naṉṟāka viḷakkukiṟatu.

jeyiṉa nūlkaḷil cantirakuptaṉ irupattiyāṇṭu (ki,mu.325-301) aracu naṭattiya piṟaku ulakavāḻkkiyil veṟuppaṭintu pattirapāku eṉṟa jepiṉa muṉivaruṉ cīṭaṉāṉa tākavum am muṉivaruṭaṉ takkaṇam ceṉṟu paṉṉiriyāṇṭu tavampurintu kaṭiciyil paṭṭiṉi viratamūlamākat tamatu uṭalik kaḷintatākavum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. micūr camstāṉattil cantirakiri eṉṟa iṭattil iv vitihyaṅkaḷik kuṟippiṭum kalveṭṭukaḷum ciṟpaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

muriya vamcattu iraṇṭāvatu aracaṉāṉa pintucāraṉ ﻿

pintucāraṉ

﻿acōkaṉuṭiya tantiyātalāl ivaṉippaṟṟip puttak kirantaṅkaḷil cila rucikaramāṉa katikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil uṇmiyit tēṭi aṟivatu kaṣṭamāyirukkiṟatu. ivaṉ kālattil āṭci muṟiyil vicēṣa māṟutalkaḷ ēṟpaṭavilliyāyiṉum rājya kāriyaṅkaḷ oḻuṅkāka naṭantēṟiṉaveṉṟum, takṣiṇam muriyariṉ ēkātipattiyattil aikkiya maṭintatu ikkālattileṉṟum kūṟuvataṟku ātāraṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. cantirakupataṉ kālattil intiyāvukkum siriyā mutaliya kuṇa tici nāṭukaḷukkum nē uṟavu ēṟpaṭṭatu; ivvuṟavu pintusāraṉ kālattilum nīṭittu vantatu. pintusāraṉ yavaṉaruṭiya viññāṉa cāstiraṅkaḷi viyantu, ivaṟṟiṉ cūkṣumattit terintukoḷḷa, siriyā tēcattu aracaṉiṭam tērnta vittuvāṉkaḷit taṉakku aṉuppik koṭukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu kēṭṭatāka oru krīk varalāṟu irukkiṉṟatu. ivaṉ irupattēḻu varuṣaṅkaḷ aracupurintu piṉ maraṇamaṭintāṉ (kālam, ki, mu. 301-273)

acōkaṉuṭiya jīviya caritikku avaṉatu cāsaṉaṅkaḷiyum ﻿

acōkaṉip

paṟṟi aitihyaṅ

kaḷiliruntu kiṭi

kkum vivaraṅkaḷ.

﻿purātaṉa ārāycciyāl teriyavantirukkum cila ceytikaḷiyum nām mukkiya ātāramāy vittuk koḷḷalām. acōkaṉippaṟṟiya aitihyaṅkaḷ iraṇṭu cārpiliruntu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. (i) tivyāvatāṉam eṉṟa samskirutak kirantattiluḷḷa vaṭa intiya aitihyam. (ii) cilōṉ tīviluḷḷa puttariṉ itikāsa kirantaṅkaḷākiya tīpavamcam, mahāvamcam eṉpaṉavaṟṟiliruntu kiṭikkum cilōṉ aitihyam. inta iru cārpu aitihyaṅkaḷiliruntu acōkaṉippaṟṟit teriyavarum ceytikaḷ ellām nām oppukkoḷḷak kūṭiyavaṉavā yirukkavilli. āṉāl avi muṟṟilum taḷḷattakkaviyumalla. intukkaḷuṭiya purāṇaṅkaḷil pūrvacarittira viṣayamāṉa pala ceytikaḷ aṭaṅkiyiruppiṉum aṅku acōkaṉuṭiya aracāṭciyippaṟṟi oru vivaramumilli. paṇṭiya aracariṉ peyarkaḷukkiṭiyil acōkaṉuṭiya peyarum iruppatu oru mēṉmiyāmō? patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappeṟṟa rājataraṅkiṇi eṉṟa kācmīra carittirattil acōkaṉuṭiya cila varalāṟukaḷ kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. kācmīra nāṭṭiṉ pala kōyilkaḷum araṇkaḷum acōkaṉāl ākkappaṭṭavi yeṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. evvitamum, namakku putta aitihyaṅkaḷi viṭa uṟutiyāṉa carittiracātaṉaṅkaḷilliyeṉa nām oppukkoḷḷavēṇṭum. cāsaṉaṅkaḷil kuṟippiṭāmalum putta aitihyaṅkaḷiliruntu maṭṭum teriya varuvatumāṉa ceytikaḷukkup piṉ purātaṉa vastu ārāycciyiliruntu attāṭci akappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. itaṟku utāraṇam piṉ kūṟuvōm*[1]. āyiṉum cāsaṉaṅkaḷil collappaṭṭiruppataṟku virōtamāyirukkiṟa vivaraṅkaḷiyum viṉōtak kaṟpaṉikaḷeṉṟu veḷippaṭiyāyt terikiṉṟa ceytikaḷiyum nām uṇmic carittiramāy oppukkoḷḷa muṭiyātu. ippaṭippaṭṭa kaṭṭukkatikaḷi iṅku virittuk kūṟuvatu aṉāvaciyameṉṟu namakkut tōṉṟukiṟatu.

acōkaṉiṉ iḷamip paruvattippaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ yāvum aitihyaṅkaḷil maṭṭumē uḷḷaṉa, acōkaṉ pintusāraṉiṉ mūttamakaṉalla. paṭṭattukku atika pāttiyatiyuṭiya pala cakōtarar avaṉukkiruntaṉar. āyiṉum avaṉatu putti nuṭpamum cāmarttiyamum paṟṟi avaṉukku mukkiyamāṉa aracāṅka atikāraṅkaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṉ mutalil takṣacilā eṉṟa mākāṇattiṉ atipaṉāyc cilakālam vēli ceytapiṉ ujjeyiṉiyiṉ atipaṉāka niyamikkappaṭṭāṉ. pintusāraṉ tīēkaviyōkamāṉavuṭaṉ acōkaṉ pāṭalipuratti aṭintu uparājaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭattuṭaṉ rājyattiṉ talimiyi vakikka ārampittāṉ, nāṉku varuṣaṅkaḷukkuppiṉ acōkaṉ paṭṭāpiṣēkam ceytu koṇṭāṉ.

ic ceytikaḷi nām nampalām; āṉāl acōkaṉ ciṅkātaṉattik kippaṟṟuvataṟkut toṇṇūṟṟoṉpatu cakōtarari avar maṉivi makkaḷuṭaṉ koli ceytāṉeṉṟum jaṉaṅkaḷip pala vitattil kacakkip piḻintāṉeṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟa katikaḷ uṇmiyeṉṟu tōṉṟavilli. iv viṣayaṅkaḷ acōkaṉuṭiya kuṇattippaṟṟic cāsaṉaṅkaḷip paṭippatiliruntu namakku ēṟpaṭum apippirāyattukku muṟṟilum muraṇpaṭukiṟatu. mēlum, perun tuṣṭaṉāṉa oruvaṉ tiṭīreṉṟu veku puṇṇiyavāṉāy viṭuvatu iyaṟkiyalla. paktiyiṉ aṉukuṇattāl maṉitaṉiṉ uḷḷam cīraṭiyumeṉpatu uṇmiyē. acōkaṉum puttaruṭiya cuvicēṣatti'c ceviyuṟṟatiṉāl pala paḻiya tīya vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum cupāvaṅkaḷiyum tavirttut tarmātmāvāy māṟiyirukkalām.

āṉāl acōkaṉ mutalil mikak keṭṭavaṉākavum koṭuṅkōl aracaṉākavum iruntāṉ eṉpatum piṉ tiṭīreṉṟu periya muṉicirēṣṭaṉāṉāṉ eṉpatum iyaṟkiyalla. carittira campavaṅkaḷ aitihyaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭum poḻutu avi ivvitam tirintupōkiṉṟaṉa, orē oru aracaṉ, mika mūrkkaṉākavum keṭṭavaṉākavum iruntavaṉ, tarmattic ciravaṇam ceyta māttirattil saṉmārkattiṉ uruvē pōṉṟa paricutta maṉitaṉāy viṭṭāṉeṉṟa ceyti tarmattiṉ mēṉmiyiyum kuṇattiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭa, evvaḷavu nērttiyāṉa kati! tarmattip pōtikka vēṇṭumeṉṟa uttēcattuṭaṉ tokukkappaṭṭa itikācaṅkaḷukku ik katikaḷ evvaḷavu ucitamāyirukkiṉṟaṉa ! carittira campavaṅkaḷ tarmīya nōkkattuṭaṉ eḻutappaṭum purāṇaṅkaḷil ivvita māṟutal aṭital iyaṟkiyallavā?

ik kāraṇaṅkaḷpaṟṟi nammuṭiya muṭivu eṉṉaveṉṟāl, acōkaṉ taṉ aracāṭciyi ārampitta poḻutu koṭuṅkōlaṉāyirāmal taṉatu vīraparākramattil nampikkiyum vēṭṭiyāṭutal mutaliya viyāyāmaṅkaḷil apilāṣiyum ayal nāṭukaḷi jayikka vēṇṭumeṉṟa nōkkamumuṭiya cātāraṇa aracaṉāyiruntāṉ. putta pakavāṉuṭiya kāṟṟu avaṉmēl appōtu aṭikkātatāl ākāra viṣayamākavum teyva ārātaṉikkākavum aracaṉuṭiya araṇmaṉiyil pirāṇivati naṭantu vantatu[2]. acōkaṉuṭiya patiṉāṉkāvatu paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattil eḻutappaṭṭa aintām cāsaṉattiliruntu aracaṉuṭiya cakōtara cakōtarikaḷ appōtu vāḻntu vantaṉareṉṟu nām aṟikiṟōm. ataṉāl acōkaṉ taṉatu uṟaviṉar yāvariyum koliceyta vivaram nampattakkatalla. pintusāraṉ iṟantatum acōkaṉ uṭaṉē aṭutta aracaṉāvataṟkut taṭi yiruntatu uṇmiyāyirukkalām; cakōtararkaḷukkiṭiyil caṇṭi caccaravukaḷ naṭantirukkalām; ivvaḷavu tāṉ nām aitihyattiliruntu kirakikkak kūṭiyatu. aracurimi kipaṟṟiyatik kuṟittuḷḷa maṟṟa vivaraṅkaḷ uṇmiyeṉat tōṉṟavilli.

acōkaṉ nālu varuṣam iḷavaracaṉāka aracāṭci ﻿

aracaṉiṉ

paṭṭāpiṣēkam

﻿naṭattiyapiṉ taṉakkup paṭṭāpiṣēkam ceytukoṇṭāṉ. ‘piyatasi muṭicūṭiya ittaṉi varuṣaṅkaḷāṉa piṉ’ eṉṟu pala cāsaṉaṅkaḷil aracaṉ kālattil naṭanta pala campavaṅkaḷukkuk kālam kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ataṉāl acōkaṉ karuttuppaṭit tāṉ paṭṭāpiṣēkam ceytukoṇṭa tiṉamē taṉatu āṭciyiṉ ārampatiṉam pōlum. at tiṉattil varuṣā varuṣam uṟcavam koṇṭāṭappaṭṭatu. appōtu maraṇa taṇṭaṉi vitikkappeṟṟa kuṟṟavāḷikaḷil cilari viṭutaliceyvatu vaḻakkam.[3]



acōkaṉuṭiya jīviya caritiyil kaliṅka yuttam ﻿

kaliṅka yuttam

﻿mika mukkiyamāṉa campavamākum. avaṉuṭiya oṉpatāvatu paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattil aracaṉ kaliṅka tēcattik kippaṟṟuvataṟkākap paṭiyeṭuttāṉ. taṉatu ēkātipattiyam imayamali mutal teṟkē pālāṟu variyum iṭiviṭāmal toṭarntiruppataṟkuk kaliṅkar nāṭu maṭṭum vilakkāyiruntatu. ip piratēcam oru mūliyiliruntatiṉālum aṭarttiyāṉa kāṭukaḷāl cūḻappaṭṭiruntatiṉālum atu acōkaṉiṉ muṉṉōrāl ēkātipattiyattōṭu cērkkappaṭavilli. ik kuṟiyit tīrppataṟku yuttam ārampikkappaṭṭatu. acōkaṉuṭiya eṇṇamum kikūṭiṟṟu. āṉāl avaṉ maṟṟa aracarippōl taṉatu veṟṟiyil cantōṣamaṭiyavilli. yuttattukku iyaṟkiyāṉa koliyum koṭumiyum kaṣṭaṅkaḷum avaṉ maṉattil tikilic ceytaṉa. periyatōṣam ceytōmē yeṉṟu avaṉukku ēkkam piṭittatu. cuyamākavē acōkaṉ irakkamuḷḷavaṉ ; atōṭukūṭa itē camayattil aracaṉ putta tarmattik kēṭkavum nērntatu. avaṉ tāṉ paṭum paccāttāpattip patiṉmūṉṟām cāsaṉattil kaṇṇuṅ kaṇṇīrumāy eḻutivittirukkiṟāṉ. evvaḷavu periya lāpattukkāyiṉum iṉimēl oru pōtum yuttam ceyvatilliyeṉṟu acōkaṉ piratikñi ceytāṉ. appiratikñi kāppāṟṟappaṭṭatu avaṉatu niṣkapaṭamāṉa cupāvatti viḷakkukiṟatu.

eppaṭiyāyiṉum kaliṅkatēcam jayikkappaṭṭatu; naṭantupōṉa kāriyam avaṉāl māṟṟakkūṭiyatā yirukkavilli. putitāka jayikkappaṭṭa jaṉaṅkaḷip pātukāppataṟkum avarkaḷiṉ arukāmiyiluḷḷa kāṭṭu jātiyāric camrakṣaṇi ceyvataṟkum atikārikaḷukku vicēṣa eccarikki ceyyappaṭṭatu. kaliṅka tēcattu atikārikaḷukku iṭappaṭṭirukkum kaṭṭaḷi iṉṉateṉṟu kaliṅka cāsaṉaṅkaḷ eṉṟa likitaṅkaḷiliruntu nām aṟintu koḷḷalām.

acōkaṉ muṭicūṭiya oṉpatāmāṇṭu mutal avaṉukkup ﻿

putta matat

tiṉ sparcam

﻿putta tarmattil pakticratti atikarittukkoṇṭē vantatu. im māṟutalukkuk kaliṅka yuttattiṉ koṭumi maṭṭumalla kāraṇameṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. upakuptaṉ eṉṟa tuṟavi aracaṉukkup puttariṉ cuvicēṣatti upatēcittāreṉṟu vaṭa intiya aitihyaṅkaḷ collukiṉṟaṉa. cukanta vastukkaḷ ceyyum kuptaṉ makaṉ upakupta ṉeṉṟu ivarpeyar viritturikkappaṭukiṟatu. ittuṟavi vivēkamum paricuttamum niṟintiruntāreṉṟum puttarukkuppiṉ putta caṅkattiṉ talimi stāṉatti vakitta ācāriyaruḷ aintāvatāka niṉṟāreṉṟum, vekunāḷ acōkaṉukkuk kuruvākavum tuṇivarākavum vāḻntu vantāreṉṟum collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki. pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil intiyāvil cañcārañceyta yuvaṉcuvaṅ eṉṟa cīṉa yāttirikaṉ ittuṟaviyiṉ ñāpakac ciṉṉaṅkaḷākak kaṭṭap paṭṭa vihāraṅkaḷ hintu tēcattilum vaṭa maturiyilum ceḻittiruntaṉa veṉṟu kūṟukiṟāṉ. cilōṉ aitihyattil ivar peyar mōkalī puttira tissaṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.

acōkaṉ putta tarmattil īṭupaṭṭu mutalil upāsakaṉ eṉṟa nilimiyik kikkoṇṭāṉ. upāsakaṉ eṉpatu illaṟattil oḻukum puttarukkup peyar. iraṇṭari varuṣaṅkaḷukkuppiṉ, atāvatu aracaṉ muṭicūṭiya paṉṉiraṇṭām varuṣattil, avaṉ kāṭṭiya ūkkam taṉakkē tiruptikaramāyirukkavilli. muṉṉilum atika ūkkattuṭaṉ tarmattit taḻuviyoḻuka vēṇṭumeṉṟu avaṉ niccayittāṉ. ippaṭi oru varuṣamāṉa piṉ aracaṉ putta saṅkattiyē avalampittāṉ. itu nikaḻntatu aracaṉuṭiya patiṉmūṉṟām paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattil, ivvaruṣamum itaṟku muṉṉum piṉṉumuḷḷa varuṣaṅkaḷum acōkaṉiṉ - jīviya caritiyil mikavum mukkiyamāṉavi. avaṉuṭiya ellāc cīrtiruttaṅkaḷum naṉ muyaṟcikaḷum ivvāṇṭukaḷil ārampikkappaṭṭaṉa. araṇmaṉic camiyal aṟiyil māmica vaki tavirkkappaṭṭatu jīva imsiyum pirāṇivatiyum rājat turōkamākak karutappaṭumeṉṟu jaṉaṅkaḷ eccarikki ceyyappaṭṭaṉar. taṉatu tarmattiṉ koḷkikaḷik kaṟkaḷilum stampaṅkaḷilum eḻuti avaṟṟi ellōrukkum teriyappaṭuttavēṇṭumeṉṟu aracaṉ niccayittāṉ. vēṭṭiyāṭutal mutaliya paḻiya poḻutu pōkkukaḷ tavirkkappaṭṭaṉa. ataṟkup patilāka avaṉ puṇṇiya stala yāttirikaḷ ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. āṅkāṅkuḷḷa ñāṉacirēṣṭarkaḷit taricippatum, tarmattip pōtippatum, tarma vivātaṅkaḷi naṭattuvatumē aracaṉuṭiya poḻutupōkkāyiṟṟu, aracaṉuṭiya vāḻnāṭkaḷil ippaṭippaṭṭa puṉita yāttirikaḷ pala ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.

ivaṟṟil piratāṉamāṉatu acōkaṉiṉ irupattōrāvatu ﻿

⁠tīrtta

yāttirikaḷ

﻿paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattil naṭanta yāttiri. pāṭalipurattiliruntu puttariṉ cutēcamākiya kapilavastuvukkuc cellum pātiyil ippōtu ēḻu stampaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. inta stampaṅkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟil acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟiraṇṭil oruvita likitamumilli. inta stampaṅkaḷ muṉ collappaṭṭa tīrttayāttiriyiṉ ñāpakac ciṉṉaṅkaḷeṉṟu nām ūkikkiṟōm. aitihyattiṉ paṭi acōkaṉ inta yāttiriyil upakuptaṉiyum aḻittuc ceṉṟāṉ pōlum, avarkaḷ mutalil puttariṉ piṟappiṭamākiya lumpiṉi kirāmattit taricittārkaḷām ; piṉṉar, kapilavastu nakarattiyum, puttar pūrṇañāṉam aṭinta iṭamākiya puttakayāviyum, ammakāṉ tamatu tarma cakkarattic cuḻaṟṟa ārampitta sārnātattiyum, avar kaṭiciyāy parinirvāṇamaṭinta kucinakarattiyum, avar tarmap piracāram ceytukoṇṭu palakālam vacittuvanta srīnāvastiyiyum ivikaḷallāmal vēṟu iraṇṭu kṣēttiraṅkaḷiyum avarkaḷ taricittārkaḷām. puttariṉ piṟappiṭamākiya lumpiṉi taṟkālam rummiṉtēyi eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. inta kṣēttiratti acōkaṉ taricittatiṉ aṟikuṟiyāynāṭṭappaṭṭa stampam, veku kālamāka maṟintu kiṭantatu; atu camīpakālattil tirumpavum uttāraṇam ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. acōkaṉ inta ūrilirunta jaṉaṅkaḷukku nilattīrviyik kuṟittāṉeṉṟu rummiṉ tēyi stampattiluḷḷa likitam namakkut terivikkiṉṟatu. acōkaṉ itē camayattil koṉākamaṉaṉ eṉṟa pūrvaputtariṉ kṣēttirattit taricaṉam ceytu, aṅkirunta stūpattip putuppittu oru stampattiyum nāṭṭiya ceytikaḷ aitihyattil kūṟappaṭavilliyāyiṉum cilācāsaṉa paricōtaṉi mūlamāka nām terintukoḷḷukiṟōm. icceytikaḷ eḻutappaṭṭirukkum stampamum rummiṉtēyiṉ arukiliruntu namakku akappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ivvivaraṅkaḷ acōkaṉatu tarmam ettaṉmiyatu eṉṟu tīrmāṉikka upayōkamāṉavi.

nēppāḷattiṉ talinakaramākiya kātmāṇṭu allatu laḷitāpaṭṭaṇam acōkaṉāl stāpikkappaṭṭateṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. aṅku aracaṉ pala kōyilkaḷ kaṭṭiṉāṉeṉṟum aracaṉ makaḷ cārumati avaṉuṭaṉ aṅkuc ceṉṟu taṉ takappaṉāriṉ vēlikku utaviyāyiruntu piṉ aṅkēyē nilittiruntāḷeṉṟum aitihyattil kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭṭaṇam rummiṉtēyiyiṉ arukiluḷḷatāl innakarattiṉ stāpaṉam rummiṉtēyi likitattil kuṟippiṭṭirukkum aracaṉiṉ irupattoṉṟām paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattilēyē naṭantirukkalām.

taṉatu paṉṉiraṇṭāvatu paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattil aracaṉ ﻿

⁠tuṟavi aracu

naṭattiya vinti

﻿putta saṅkattic cērntatāl avaṉ canniyāsi āṉāṉ. kaṭicivariyum avaṉ inta āciramattiliruntu māṟavilli pōlum. ki. pi. ēḻāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil intiyāvil cañcaritta itsiṅ, tuṟaviyuṭiyaṇinta acōkap piratimiyik kaṇṭatāka eḻutukiṟāṉ. āṉāl aitihyaṅkaḷilum cāsaṉaṅkaḷilum acōkaṉuṭiya maṉivimārkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. pikṣūvāṉapiṉ taṉ rājya kāriyaṅkaḷi acaṭṭiceytāṉeṉṟum vaṭaitihyattil kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. taliyic cirittuk , kāṣāyavastiramuṭutta aracaṉ eppaṭi ciṅkātaṉattil vīṟṟiruntāṉeṉṟum rājya kāriyaṅkaḷik kavaṉittāṉeṉṟum namakkum caṅki uṇṭākiṟatu. acōkaṉuṭiya cāsaṉaṅkaḷ inta camcayatti muṟṟilum nivirtticeykiṉṟaṉa . “eṉatu kaṭamiyāka ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa vēliyil nāṉ kāṭṭum jākkirati eṉakku orupōtum tiruptikaramākiṟatilli. sarva jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ apivirttikkāka catā uḻippatu eṉ kaṭami.” “evviṭattilum jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻakkukaḷi vicāraṇi ceyya nāṉ tayārāyirukkiṟēṉ.” ippaṭip pēcum aracaṉ taṉ kaṭamiyi acaṭṭi ceytāṉeṉṟu colvati nampuvatu eppaṭi? tuṟavaṟamum ulaka naṉmikkuriya piravirttiyum oṉṟukkoṉṟu muraṇallaveṉṟu pakavatkītiyil vaṟpuṟuttappaṭum koḷkiyē acōkaṉ koḷkipōlum.



mēlum, cāsaṉaṅkaḷil aracaṉuṭiya rāṇikaḷiyum piḷḷikaḷiyumpaṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. tuṟaviyāṉa piṉ aracaṉ avarkaḷuṭaṉ vāḻavilliyeṉṟu nām aṉumāṉañceytu koṇṭālum avarkaḷuṭiya yōkakṣēmaṅkaḷi avaṉ kavaṉittuvantāṉeṉpatu teḷivu.[4] acōkaṉ kaṭicivariyum taṉatu kaṭamikaḷi niṟivēṟṟuvatil kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāyiruntāṉeṉṟu nām kūṟuvatu mikiyaṉṟu. ippaṭippaṭṭa ūkkamuṭimi, aracarukku iṉṟiyamiyātateṉṟu kuṭalyaṉuṭiya arttacāstirattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāl acōkaṉ iv viṣayattil tam muṉṉōrvaḻiyi aṉucarittu vantāṉaṉṟi vēṟilli.

saṅkattiṟ cērntapiṉ aracaṉ ūkkamuṭimi muṉṉilum ﻿

tarmap piracā

ram ceytal

﻿atika vicālamaṭintatu. turittaṉak kāriyaṅkaḷil maṭṭum taṉatu ūkkatti niṟuttik koḷḷāmal ulakameṅkum tarmappiracārañceyyavēṇṭumeṉṟa avā. acōkaṉip paṟṟiyatu. taṉatu rājyattiṉuḷḷum veḷiyilum tarmap piracāram naṭattiṉāṉ avaṉatu ēkātipattiyattukkuppaṭṭa yavaṉar, kāmpōjar, nāpakatēcattu nāpapantiyar, pōjar, pitēṉikar, āntirar, pulintar, rāṣṭirikar, kāntārar mutaliya jaṉaṅkaḷukkup pala tarma āciriyar aṉuppappaṭṭaṉar. anta jaṉaṅkaḷ acōkaṉ pōtaṉiyi aṉpuṭaṉ kēṭṭārkaḷeṉṟu nām ūkikkalām.

tarma pōtaṉikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭa piratēcaṅkaḷiṉ peyarum antanta iṭaṅkaḷukku aṉuppappaṭṭa āciriyariṉ peyarum cilōṉ aitihyattil[5] kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. kaṉṉiṅhām turi 1873-m ௵l sāñciyiṉ arukēyuḷḷa ērāḷamāṉa purātaṉa vastukkaḷic cōtaṉi ceytapōtu putta muṉivariṉ elumpukaḷ nikṣēpikkappaṭṭa pāttiraṅkaḷ akappaṭṭaṉa. iraṇṭu pāttiraṅkaḷiṉ puṟattil “himavantap piratēcattiṉ ācāriyaṉākiya kāciyapa kōttiraṉ cāmpal” eṉṟum, “himavantap piratēcattiṉ ācāriyaṉākiya majjimaṉ cāmpal” eṉṟum likitaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. makāvamcattilum ippeyarkaḷuṭiya ācāriyar acōkaṉ kālattil imayamali allatu himavantap piratēcattil tarmattip pōtittaṉareṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭiruppatum kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

suvarṇa pūmi, allatu sōvaṉa pūmi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum parmā tēcamum acōkaṉ tūtar mūlamāka putta tarmattic ceviyuṟṟateṉṟu iṅku collappaṭukiṟatu. acōkaṉuṭiya rājyattukku uṭpaṭāta vēṟu palatēcattār acōkaṉ mūlamākavē putta tarmatti aṟintaṉareṉpatu uṇmiyē. teṉ intiyāvil cutantira aracarkaḷāyirunta cōḻar pāṇṭiyar, sattiyaputtirar, kēraḷa puttirar nāṭukaḷil tarma pōtaṉikaḷum tarmīyamāṉa upakārac ceykikaḷum naṭantuvantaṉa. cilōṉ tīviluḷḷa jaṉaṅkaḷum mutaṉ mutal puttariṉ santēcattik kēṭṭatu acōkaṉ mūlamākavē. acōkaṉuṭiya cakōtaraṉ makēntiraṉum, cakōtari saṅkamittiriyum iṅku tarmap piracārañ ceyyumporuṭṭu aṉuppappaṭṭārkaḷeṉṟu itikāsaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. teṉ intiyāvilum ivar putta tarmattip paravac ceytaṉarpōlum. makēntira vihāram eṉṟa peyaruṭiya maṭam oṉṟu teṉṉāṭṭil iruntatāka yuvaṉcuvaṅ kūṟukiṟāṉ. makāvamcattiṉpaṭi iv viruvarum acōkaṉuṭiya makkaḷām.

ēṟakkuṟiya acōkaṉ kālattatu eṉṟu aṉumāṉikkappaṭum ciṟpam oṉṟil cilōṉil putta matattip piracārañceyta viṣayam kuṟippiṭṭiruppatupōl tōṉṟukiṟatu. sāñci stūpattiṉ ōr vācaliṉ uttarakkallil uyaramāṉa ōr maṉitaṉ yāṉiyiliruntu iṟaṅkuvatiyum pōtiliruṣattiṉ kiḷi ammarattiṉiṉṟu oṭikkappaṭṭu ammaṉitaṉiṭam koṭukkappaṭuvatiyum viḷakkikkāṭṭum ciṟpam oṉṟuṇṭu. pōtivirukṣattiṉ kiḷi cilōṉukku tēvar piriyaṉāṉa tissaṉ eṉṟa aracaṉ kālattil koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa ceyti cilōṉ itikāsaṅkaḷil mika vimariciyākak kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. muṉ kūṟappaṭṭa ciṟpam intac campavattiṉ aṟikuṟiyā yirukkalāmaṉṟō ?

acōkaṉāl tarmapracāram ceyyappaṭṭa nāṭukaḷuḷ mika ﻿

veku tūrat

tuḷḷa aintu

aracaruṭaṉ uṟavu.

﻿mukkiyamāṉavi aṟunūṟu yōjaṉikkappāluḷḷa aintu aracariṉ nāṭukaḷ. āciyā airōppā āppirikkāk kaṇṭaṅkaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu cantikkum piratēcaṅkaḷi āṇṭuvanta aracar ivar. iṅku kirīkku jātiyāruṭiya nākarikam mikuntiruntatu, alaksāntar eṉṟa māsiṭōṇiyā tēcattup parākrama vīraṉ ki. mu. 335-l taṉatu tikvijayatti ārampittu nūṟṟukkaṇakkāy rājyaṅkaḷi jayittu intiyāvukkum paṭiyeṭuttuvanta vivaraṅkaḷ cātāraṇamāyt terintaviyē. avvīraṉ parākkiramattāl ēṟpaṭṭa ēkātipatyam avaṉuṭiya muṭivuṭaṉ cīrkulintatu. alaksāntariṉ mukkiya pōrvīrarkaḷ appōtu anta ēkātipattiyattip paṅkiṭṭuk koṇṭaṉar. patiṉmūṉṟām cāsaṉattil kūṟiyirukkum aintu aracarkaḷiṉ rājyaṅkaḷ alaksāntariṉ ēkātipattiyattiliruntu uṇṭāṉavi. cāsaṉattil aintu aracariṉ peyar maṭṭum kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ivaruṭiya kālamum tēcamum piṉ varumāṟu:-



* * *



aintu aracaruk⁠ ivvaracariṉ ivar āṇṭu⁠ kālam

kuc cāsaṉaṅka ⁠ ⁠ ⁠

ḷil vantuḷḷa airōppiyap peyar. vanta tēcam ki.mu.
