aṉṭi yōkka⁠ iraṇṭām āṇṭi⁠ siriyā⁠ 261-246

  yākkas (tiyās)

atula māya pṭālamiyās (pi ekiptu 285-247

  lāṭelpās)

aṉtēkiṉa aṇṭikōṉas māsiṭōṇiyā 277-239

  (koṉāṭas)

maka makās kirīṉē 285-258

alaka sutara alaksāntar eppiras 272-258



* * *





siriyā tēcattukkum. mōriyariṉ ēkātipattiyattukkum selyukkas kālattiliruntē nēcapāvam iruntu vantatu. mekāstaṉis pōṉṟa yavaṉa tūtarkaḷ mōriya rājatāṉiyil yavaṉa aracariṉ kāriyaṅkaḷi nōkki vantaṉar. ataṉāl acōkaṉ inta ayal aracarukkum taṉakkum mika arumiyeṉṟu tōṉṟiya cuvicēṣattit terivittatu āccariyamilli. acōkaṉ iṅku tarmattip pōtittatuṭaṉ tiruptiyaṭiyāmal vittiyacālikaḷiyum miruka āspattirikaḷiyum stāpittu avaṟṟip paripālittu vantāṉ.

acōkaṉuṭiya cāsaṉaṅkaḷil aintu aracar kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu ﻿

itiliruntu

kiṭikkum

samakālattuvam

﻿pūrvika intiya carittirattiṉ kālanirṇayattukku mikavum mukkiyamāṉa ātāramāyirukkiṟatu. inta aintu aracarkaḷum cērntu jīvittiruntatu nāṉku varuṣaṅkaḷ maṭṭumē. it tūratēcaṅkaḷukku acōkaṉ tūtari aṉuppiyatu taṉatu paṉṉiraṇṭāvatu paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattileṉṟu cāsaṉaṅkaḷ namakkut terivikkiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl acōkaṉuṭiya paṉṉiraṇṭāvatu varuṣatti nālu varuṣaṅkaḷukku atikam muṉpiṉ pōkāmal nām niccayikkalām. ki. mu. 258-௵l acōkaṉum inta aintu aracarkaḷum orumittu jīvantarāyiruntaṉar. iti samakālattuvam (Synchronism) eṉṟu collalām. inta samakālat tuvattiliruntu acōkaṉuṭiya rājyapārakālattiyē nām tīrmāṉikkalām. itallāmal maṟṟoru samakālattuvamum pūrvika intiya carittirattukku mikavum mukkiyam ; atāvatu kirīkku pāṣiyiluḷḷa alaksāntar vīraṉ caritikaḷil santirakōṭṭās eṉṟa intiya aracaṉ peyar kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu. inta sntirakōṭṭās muttirārākṣasam eṉṟa camskiruta nāṭakattiṉ pāttiraṉāṉa cantirakuptaṉ eṉṟa mōriya vamca stāpakaṉ tāṉ eṉṟa aṉumāṉam pūrvika intiyā carittirattil mutalāvatu ēṟpaṭṭa samakālattuvam. iraṇṭāvatāka ēṟpaṭṭa samakālattuvam acōkaṉ cāsaṉaṅkaḷoṉṟil aintu tūratēcattu aracarkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭiruppatē.

acōkaṉukku aintu mēṉāṭṭu aracaruṭaṉ ēṟpaṭṭa uṟavāṉatu intiya carittira ārāyccikku ōr samakālattuvatti aḷittiruppataṉṟi vēṟu vitattilum ﻿

⁠kuṇatici

ulakam kuṭatici

ulakattic

cantittatu

﻿ mukkiyamākak kuṟippiṭa vēṇṭiya ōr viṣayamākum. puttamatamāṉatu piṉ kālaṅkaḷil kīḻāciyāvil pirapalamāṉatupōla mēl āciyāvil pirapalamācavilli. āyiṉum ippiratēcaṅkaḷilum acōkaṉukkup piṉ cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ putta camayattiṉ kāṟṟu vīciyatu. putta ālayaṅkaḷum maṭaṅkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. intiyāvukkup puṟampāṉa nāṭukaḷil kaṭumiyāṉa niyamaṅkaḷum tavamumuṭiya pala tuṟavikaḷ cañcārañ ceyvatum vacittuvaruvatum cakajamāyiṉa. itaṉāl mēṉāṭukaḷil ivvita tuṟaviccaṅkaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu, vāḻvu tuṉpamē, eṉṟa koḷkiyum, puṉarjaṉmattil nampikkiyum, pulāl uṇṇāmi yōkāppiyācaṅkaḷ mutaliya vaḻakkaṅkaḷum vekuvākap pārāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. immāṟutalkaḷukku mūlakāraṇam ippākaṅkaḷil acōkaṉ ceyta tarmappiracāramē, . kiṟistava matastāpakarākiya iyēcu kiṟistu pālastīṉ nāṭṭil avatarittapōtu aṅkē intiyaruṭiya attiyātma cāstiraṅkaḷum tuṟavaṟaṅkaḷum celvākkaṭintiruntaṉa. itiliruntu oru carittira uṇmi viḷaṅkukiṟatu. atāvatu, pūkōḷap paṭattil tūrattālum, pāṣiyiṉālum, kāṭu mali kaṭal mutaliya iyaṟkiyiṉ taṉmikaḷālum atikamākap pirikkappaṭṭa nāṭukaḷukkiṭiyilum ātikālaṅkaḷiliruntu paṇṭamāṟṟutal maṭṭumaṉṟu, eṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ koṭuttuvāṅkalum naṭantēṟiṉaveṉpatē. ippōtu intiyāvil airōppiya nākarikamum innāṭṭu nākarikamum cantit tiruppatupōla ekiptu, variyā, pālastīṉ mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷil ki. mu. mūṉṟu, iraṇṭu, oṉṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil kuṇatici ulakam kuṭatici ulakattōṭu cantittatu.

acōkaṉiṉ patiṉmūṉṟām āṇṭilum irupatām ﻿

ājīvakarkaḷuk

kuk kukikaḷ

ceytal

﻿āṇṭilum ippōtu kayiyiṉ arukil kāṇappaṭum mūṉṟu kukikaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. intak kukikaḷ kaṭuntuṟavikaḷākiya ājīvakarkaḷukku tāṉam ceyyappaṭṭavi. iti nām acōkaṉ āḷukiyil vicēṣa campavamākak koḷḷavēṇṭiyatilli. avaṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa maṟṟa pala tāṉa tarmaṅkaḷiṉ lakṣiyaṅkaḷ aḻintupōyiṉa, ivi maṭṭum nili niṟkiṉṟaṉa, avvaḷavē.

iruppattēḻāvatu iruppatteṭṭāvatu paṭṭāpiṣēka ﻿

⁠stampa

cāsaṉaṅkaḷip

piracurañceytal

﻿varuṣaṅkaḷil acōkaṉuṭiya ēḻu stampacāsaṉaṅkaḷ variyappaṭṭaṉa. acōkaṉuṭiya aracāṭciyiṉ taṉmiyi aṟintuṇara ivi mikavum mukkiyamāṉavi. ivaṟṟil ēḻāvatu stampa cāsaṉam vicēṣamākak kuṟippiṭattakkatu. itil acōkaṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa ellāc cīrtiruttaṅkaḷuṭiyavum matippuri irukkiṟatu. ivvaki likitaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa ‘tūṇkaḷ’ acōkaṉāl muṉṉamēyē nāṭṭappaṭṭirukkalām. avaṉuṭiya muṉṉōrkaḷum ippaṭippaṭṭa stampaṅkaḷi nāṭṭiyirukkalām. aracaṉ muṭicūṭiya irupattōrām varuṣattil naṭanta tīrttayāttiriyiṉ ñāpakacciṉṉamāka nāṭṭappaṭṭa stampaṅkaḷippaṟṟi muṉṉē kūṟiṉōm. cila stampaṅkaḷ mutalil kīrtti stampaṅkaḷāka nāṭṭappaṭṭirukkalām. acōkaṉ, tāṉ jaṉaṅkaḷukkut terivikkak karutiya viṣayaṅkaḷi ivaṟṟilellām eḻutavēṇṭumeṉṟu tīrmāṉittāṉ.

acōkaṉ kālattil kaṭiciyāka naṭanta campavam ﻿

putta

makā sapi

﻿mūṉṟāvatu makā capiyāyirukkavēṇṭum. cilōṉ aitihyattil inta makā capi aracaṉatu pattoṉpatāmāṇṭil naṭantatākak kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. itu cariyeṉṟu tōṉṟavilli. irupattēḻāvatu allatu irupatteṭṭāvatu paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattukku muṉ inta makā sapi naṭantiruntāl attaṉi mukkiyamāṉa campavam ēḻām stampacāsaṉattil kūṟappaṭāmalirātu. āṉatāl sapi naṭantatu ki.mu; 240-l āyirukkalām. itu evvāṇṭil naṭipeṟṟatāyiṉum, mūṉṟāvatu makā sapi putta camayattiṉ vaḷarcciyil mikavum mukkiyamāṉa tuṟi eṉpatil aiyamilli. iti viḷakkuvataṟkup putta saṅkattiṉ muṉ carittiram caṟṟu uṇarvatu avaciyam, iccaṅkaṅkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟi aṭutta atikārattil eṭutturippōm.

cāsaṉaṅkaḷil piratipalittuk kāṇum tōṟṟamē ﻿

acōkaṉ

kuṇam

﻿acōkaṉuṭiya uṇmiyāṉa uruvameṉṟu muṉṉē kūṟiṉōm. iṅku aracaṉuṭiya koḷkikaḷum maṉamum paḷiṅkiṟpōl viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa ; avaṉ uḷḷattil tuṟaviyiṉ pakti cirattiyum aracaṉiṉ ñāṉamum ūkkamum oruṅkē kalakkiṉṟaṉa. maṉitaṉākak kaṇittālum aracaṉākak kaṇittālum acōkaṉ mēṉmi kuṉṟuvatilli. aracaṉākak kaṇikkumiṭattu, acōkaṉuṭiya pōr ceyyāviratamum mataṅkaḷ pāl camamāṉa pārviyum vicēṣamākak kuṟippiṭattakkaṉavām. srī. viṉseṉṭ smit eṉṟa piracittipeṟṟa intiya carittira āciriyar akpariyum acōkaṉiyum cīrtūkki eḻutukiṟār.[6] mataṅkaḷ viṣayattil ivvirumaṉṉariṉ camatiruṣṭiyum eccamayattārukkum oṉṟu pōl curanta aṉpu mātaravum meccattakkaṉavē. ivviṣayattil ivviru cuṭarkaḷiṉ oḷiyum nikareṉiṉum, puṟaccamayaṅkaḷip paḻippavariṭiyil tōṉṟi vaḷarnta akparuṭiya camatiruṣṭi acōkaṉativiṭa vicālamum perumiyumuṭiyateṉa nām oppukkoḷḷalām. āyiṉum, muṉ kūṟiya āciriyar kuṟippiṭuvatupōla, akpar puttikku yuttaṅkaḷit tavirttu tarmattimaṭṭum paravacceyvateṉpatu pētimiyākat tōṉṟumēyaṉṟi vēṟilli. acōkaṉō, ayal nāṭukaḷik kippaṟṟuvatiṉ koṭumiyit taṉatu āḷukiyiṉ toṭakkattilēyē uṇarntāṉ. akpar acōkaṉuṭiya patiṉmūṉṟāvatu cāsaṉattip paṭittiruntāl avaṉip payaṅkoḷḷiyākavē karutiyiruppar. aracar pūmiyil pērāciyit tavirkkavēṇṭumeṉpatu akpar puttikkē eṭṭavilli. ikkacappāṉa ariya uṇmi taṟkālattil pala rājāṅka nipuṇarkaḷāl oppukkoḷḷappaṭukiṟatu, ivvuṇmiyi uṇarntatē acōka rājaṉuṭiya aḻiyāp pukaḻām.

aracaṉāyiruntum maṉitaṉukkuriya lakṣiyaṅkaḷi maṟakkātu, cuka pōkaṅkaḷil mayaṅkātu vāḻntuvanta maṉṉar mikac cilarē. carittiramūlamākiya ātāraṅkaḷil mikunta vittiyācamuḷḷapōtilum, kapaṭamaṟṟa uḷḷattiṟkum ellōr kāṭcikkum eḷiyaṉāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa ārvattiṟkum taṉatu kaṭamikaḷil ōyākkavaṉattiṟkum aṟaneṟiyit taḻuvum cirattikkum acōkaṉi rōmarājyattiṉ cakkaravarttiyāṉa mārkkas oḷareliyas eṉṟa makāṉuṭaṉ oppiṭalām. ūṉ tavirttal, kollāmi, āṭamparamaṟṟa vāḻkki mutaliya nōṉpukaḷik kikkoṇṭavarum tuṟaviyāka aracu naṭattiyavarum cilaruṇṭu. āṉāl avar maṟṟappaṭi acōkaṉukku oppākārātaliṉ avari iṅkuk kuṟippiṭavēṇṭiya avaciyamilli. avaṉ putta nūlkaḷil arukaṉ eṉṟa āṉmīya nilimiyi aṭintāṉeṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. acōkaṉ oru makā puruṣaṉeṉṟu nāmum kūṟalām. appaṭiyiruntum avaṉ karvamaṭintatākat tōṉṟavilli. avaṉuṭiya karvamiṉmikku pāprucāsaṉam cāṉṟākum. tāṉ rājātirājaṉāyiruntum avvūriluḷḷa pikṣukkaḷukku, eḻutiyirukkum kaṭitattil aṭicaṉ taṉatu namaskāratti avarkaḷukkuc camarppittu eḻutattoṭaṅkukiṟāṉ. āṉma paricīlaṉi evarukkum avaciyameṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttum mūṉṟām stampacāsaṉamum iṅku pirattiyēkamākak kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ivvita uyarnta cupāvaṅkaḷ amintavaṉāyiruntum akkālattup pāmara jaṉaṅkaḷukku acōkaṉuṭiya mēṉmiyiviṭa, avaṉatu maṭami tāṉ naṉṟākap pulappaṭṭatu. naṉmiyil nampikkiyaṟṟa ulakamāṉatu, aracaṉ aṟaneṟiyip paravacceyvatil koṇṭa uṟcākattiyum ellōrum naṉkaṟinta naṟpōtaṉikaḷi vaṟpuṟuttuvatil kāṭṭiya ūkkattiyum avaṉuṭiya pētimikkut tiruṣṭāntamākak koṇṭatu. avaṉ taṉakkup perumiyeṉak karutiya tēvāṉāmpiriyaṉ eṉṟa virutāṉatu piṟ﻿kālaṅkaḷil camskiruta moḻiyil mūṭaṉeṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkuvatum viyakkattakkatē.

acōkaṉuṭiya vicālamāṉa nōkkaṅkaḷum eṇṇaṅkaḷum kikūṭavilli. āyiṉum, itu aracaṉiṉ kuṟṟameṉṟu kūṟuvatu tavaṟu. muyaṟci maṭṭumē maṉitaṉukku uriyatu. eṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ veṟṟi allatu tōlvi iyaṟkiyiluḷḷa vēṟu pala nimittaṅkaḷip poṟuttirukkiṟatu. ivi ēkōpittāl maṉitaṉiṉ nōkkaṅkaḷ kikūṭukiṉṟaṉa. atiṉāl acōkaṉuṭiya eṇṇaṅkaḷ kikūṭavilliyeṉpatu avaṉ pukaḻik kuṟikkavilli. tarmattip pōṟṟuvatil ēṟpaṭṭa cirattiyilum, mataviṣayamāṉa caṇṭikaḷ ōyntu maṭintupōkavēṇṭumeṉṟa nōkkattilum, jaṉaṅkaḷ oṟṟumiyuṭaṉum cupikṣattuṭaṉum cantōṣattuṭaṉum vāḻntuvaravēṇṭumeṉṟa kōrikkiyilum, jaṉaṅkaḷuṭiya kṣēmam aracaṉuṭiya naṭattiyip poṟuttirukkiṟateṉṟa uṇmiyiṉ uṇarcciyilum acōkaṉuṭiya cariti māṉiṭarukkup periya maliyilakkāyirukkiṟatu.

nāṭṭiṉ kṣēmattip palavāṟu vaḷarac ceytu acōkaṉ ﻿

acōkaṉukkup

piṉ vanta

mōriya aracar

﻿muppattēḻu varuṣaṅkaḷ āṇṭapiṉ kālakatiyi aṭintāṉ, aracaṉuṭiya iṟutik kālam ki. mu. 232 eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭappaṭukiṟatu. acōkaṉukkup pala kumārar iruntaṉar. makatatēcattil acōkaṉ pēraṉāṉa tacarataṉ, acōkaṉukkup piṉ aracaṉāṉāṉ, ivaṉ taṉ mūtāti ceytuḷḷa kukikaḷiṉ arukil ājīvakarkaḷukku mūṉṟu kukikaḷic ceytirukkiṟāṉ. itu tāṉ ivaṉippaṟṟi namakkut terintatu. sampirati eṉṟa peyaruṭiya acōkaṉiṉ maṟṟoru pēraṉ jayiṉamatattip paripālimāka anta matattārukkiṭiyil perumpukaḻ peṟṟāṉeṉa jayiṉak kirantaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. acōkaṉuṭiya santatiyār avaṉatu tarmacintiyiyō tiṟamiyiyō piṉpaṟṟavilli. taṉ santatiyārukkeṉṟu cāsaṉaṅkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭirukkum eccarikkikaḷ kavaṉikkap paṭavilliyeṉṟu nām ūkikkalām. purāṇaṅkaḷiluḷḷa vamcāvaḷiyiṉ paṭi acōkaṉukkuppiṉ vanta muriya aracar, aṟuvar. avar makatarājyatti ki. mu. 232 mutal 185-௵ variyum āṇṭu vantaṉar. appōtu ēkātipattiyattiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷ vēṟupaṭṭu cutantira rājyaṅkaḷāy māṟiṉa. ituvaṉṟi, ivvaracarip paṟṟi namakkut terintatum koñcamē. avaṟṟi iṅkē eṭuttuccollavēṇṭiya avaciyamumilli. kaṭiciyāka piruhatrataṉ eṉṟa mōriyaṉiṭamiruntu puṣyamittiraṉ eṉṟa cuṅka vamcattu aracaṉ rājya lakṣmiyik kippaṟṟik koṇṭāṉ. ataṟkup piṉṉum muriyar pāṭalīpurattil ciṟṟaracarāy vāḻntaṉar.



↑ kīḻ 20- m pakkam pārkka ,

↑ mutaṟcāsaṉam.

↑ aintām stampa cāsaṉam.

↑ aintām cācaṉam.

↑ makāvamcam, at. 12.

↑ The Oxford History of India by V. A. Smith p. 115.





kuṟippukaḷ





III. acōkaṉ tarmam



acōkaṉāl kūṟappaṭum tarmam eppaṭippaṭṭatu, puttariṉ matattip piracāram ceyvatil avaṉ evvaḷavu tūram payaṉaṭintāṉ eṉṟa viṣayaṅkaḷi nām taṉiyāka ārāyvatu avaciyam. putta matattiṉ vaḷarcciyil maṭṭumalla, intiyāviṉ samaya carittirattil acōkaṉuṭiya stāṉam mika mukkiyamāṉatu,

acōkaṉ mataṅkaḷukkellām sarva cammatamāṉa kālaṅkamārkkattiyē tarmam eṉṟa colliṉāl ﻿

⁠cōkaṉ

kālattil

peḷatta matat

til ēṟpaṭṭa

māṟutalkaḷ

﻿kuṟikkiṟāṉ. āyiṉum avaṉ puttaruṭiya kālattil matattil vicēṣa paṟṟuḷḷavaṉāyiruntāṉeṉpatil aiyamilli. avaṉ eṭṭuppaṭikaḷuṭiya sampōti eṉṟa ñāṉamārkkatti vaḻipaṭṭatāka ōriṭattil kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu (eṭṭām cāsaṉam). maṟṟōriṭattil aracaṉ puttamatattiṉ mummaṇiyākiya puttaṉ, tarmam, saṅkam eṉpatit tāṉ caraṇamaṭivatākac collappaṭukiṟatu (pāpru cāsaṉam). itē cāsaṉattil aracaṉ cila puttak kirantaṅkaḷ vicēṣa oḷiyuṭiyaṉaveṉṟu tāṉ matippatākak kūṟukiṟāṉ, oṉpatāvatu paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattil aracaṉ puttariṉ koḷki mikac cirēṣṭameṉṟu uṇarntu patiṉmūṉṟām āṇṭil saṅkattil cērntukoṇṭa vivaraṅkaḷi muṉ coṉṉōm.

aracaṉ putta saṅkattiṉ ōr amcamāṉatum atupaṟṟi avaṉukkup putta saṅkattiṉ talimi stāṉam ēṟpaṭṭateṉṟu nām ūkikkalām. putta makā sapikaḷil ellāruṭiya vākkum camamāṉa matippuḷḷateṉṟālum, acōkaṉ taṉatu ēkātipattiyattiṉ celvattiyum celvākkiyum putta saṅkattukku urimiyākkiyatāl avaṉukku saṅkattil vicēṣa kuravam ēṟpaṭṭirukkavēṇṭum. aracaṉiṉ cakōtara cakōtarikaḷum puttirarum puttirikaḷum saṅkattic cērntaṉareṉṟu aitihyattil collappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉāl putta matattukku atika makimiyuṇṭāyiṟṟu. ippōtu pūmiyil putta matam kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ matamāyiruppatu acōkaṉuṭiya uḻippiṉṟi naṭantirātu. puttariṉ maraṇattukkup piṉ avaruṭiya saṅkam koñcaṅ koñcamāka vaṭa intiyāvil paravi vantatu. acōkaṉatu uḻippiṉ payaṉāka atu intiyā muḻumikkum mukkiya matamāyiṟṟu. intiyāvukkup puṟamēyum āyirakkaṇakkāka jaṉaṅkaḷ puttariṉ matattic caraṇamaṭintaṉar. 'eṅkum tarmattiṉ jayapēriki muḻaṅkiṟṟu. pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka orupōtum campaviyāta vicēṣam piyataṣi aracaṉ tarmattip piṉpaṟṟiyatāl campavittatu tarmattiṉ veṟṟi eṅkum teriyavantatu' (aintām cāsaṉam) ivvacaṉaṅkaḷ taṉatu kālattukkup piṉ putta matam aṭiyappōkum kīrttiyiyum pukaḻiyum taṉatu akak kaṇṇāl aracaṉ uṇarntu kūṟiyavipōlirukkiṉṟaṉa.

aracaṉukku saṅkattiṉ talimi stāṉam ēṟpaṭṭatāl pala vicēṣak kaṭamikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa, saṅkattiṉ kāvalaṉāka avaṉ atil kaṭcikaḷum koḷki vēṟupāṭukaḷum varāmal kākkavēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. ācāraṅkaḷik kāppāṟṟavum, puttariṉ vākkiyaṅkaḷ. eviyeṉṟu tīrmāṉañceyyavum, matakkoḷkikaḷi ārāyavum aracaṉ oru makāsapi kūṭṭiya viṣayam muṉṉamē coṉṉōm. atallāmal, piṣukkaḷi eccarikki ceytal, stūpaṅkaḷik kaṭṭuvittal, vihāraṅkaḷip pātukāttal, kāṭṭujātiyārukkut tarmattip pōtittal, nāṭṭiluḷḷa putta saṅkaṅkaḷip paripālittal, ataṉ kiḷikaḷil caṇṭi caccaravukaḷit tavirttal iviyellām aracaṉ kavaṉikkavēṇṭiya viṣayaṅkaḷāyiṉa.

acōkaṉ kālattukku muṉṉuḷḷa puttacaṅka capikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟi iṅkē curukkik kūṟuvatu takum.

kutama puttar vāḻntirunta nāḷaḷavum avartāmē putta ﻿

putta caṅka

capikaḷ

﻿saṅkattiṉ atipatiyākavum kuruvākavum ellā puttat tuṟavikaḷukkum maliyilakkākavum iruntār. avar saṅkattic cērntōruṭiya naṭiyuṭipāvaṉikaḷi nērvaḻiyil niṟuttap pala vitikaḷi ēṟpaṭuttiṉārāyiṉum tuṟavikaḷiṉ cutantiratti aṉāvaciyamāyk kaṭṭuppaṭuttavilli. pikṣukkaḷ catā ācāriyarkaḷi vaḻipaṭṭu avarkaḷukkuk kīḻppaṭintu naṭappatu tāṉ naṉṟeṉṟu kutamar niṉikkavilli. evarum tāmē tamakku vaḻikāṭṭiyāyirukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu avar tamatu caramōpatēcattil ellōriyum vaṟpuṟuttukiṟār. saṅkattic cērntōr yāvariyum oru camūkattārākak kaṭṭuppaṭuttavēṇṭumeṉṟu avar muyaṟci ceyyavilli ; āyiṉum oṟṟumi uṇarcciyi uṇṭu paṇṇakkūṭiya ēṟpāṭukaḷ saṅkattil illāmalirukkavilli. taṅkaḷukkiṭiyil paraspara vekumāṉamum nampikkiyum vaḷaruvataṟkāka “pāṭimōkham" eṉṟa caṭaṅku ovvoru purṇamiyaṉṟum naṭantu vantatu, purṇamiyaṉṟu iravu ōriṭattil taṅkiyirukkum pikṣukkaḷ ellōruṅkūṭi taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ naṭattiyil campavitta kuṟṟaṅ kuṟivukaḷi maṟṟōrukkuk kūṟi maṉṉippuk kēṭṭuk koḷvatu vaḻakkam; itaṉṟi vēṟu vitattil, oru tuṟavi maṟṟat tuṟavikkuk kīḻppaṭiyavēṇṭiya avaciyamirukkavilli.

kutamaputtar vāḻntirunta nāḷaḷavum, avaruṭiya aṭutta cīṣarkaḷ kālattilum puttariṉ perumiyum paricuttamum putta saṅkattip piḷavukaḷiliruntu kāppāṟṟiṉa. kālakkiramattil inta oṟṟumi uṇarcciyiṉ vali kuṉṟiyatu, nūṟu varuṣaṅkaḷukkuḷ putta saṅkamāṉatu paṉmiyilēyē, ‘ saṅkaṅkaḷ,' eṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭiya rītiyil pala pirivukaḷāy māṟiṟṟu, pala piramukarkaḷ inta stitiyik kaṇṭu viyacaṉamaṭintaṉar. koḷki vēṟupāṭukaḷi nīkkavum jīvitattil oṟṟumiyiyum oḻuṅkiyum ēṟpaṭuttavum pala muyaṟcikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ makā sapikaḷik kūṭṭiyatu mikavum mukkiyamāṉatu.

mutalāvatu makāsapi puttar iṟantavuṭaṉ rājakkiruham eṉṟa nakaril naṭipeṟṟatu. puttar caramāriyāyp poḻinta tarmōpatēcaṅkaḷiyum nuṭpavātaṅkaḷiyum iṉiya campāṣaṇikaḷiyum ulakam oru pōtum iḻantuviṭāvaṇṇam kāppāṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟu sapi tīrmāṉañ ceytatu. ivaṟṟit tiraṭṭuvataṟkāka puttaruṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻakiya cīṣarkaḷ terinteṭukkappaṭṭaṉar. putta tarmattiṉ tattuvaṅkaḷippaṟṟi appoḻutē apippirāya pētaṅkaḷ. ēṟpaṭṭaṉaveṉṟu nām aṉumāṉikka iṭamirukkiṟatu. āṉāl avi pakiraṅkamākavilli. saṅkatti oṉṟākak kaṭṭuvataṟkēṟṟa ēṟpāṭukaḷum cila ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. sapiyāṉatu ellā pikṣukkaḷum aṉucarikkalēṇṭiya caṭṭaṅkaḷip piracurañceytatu. saṅkattil cērppataṟkuḷḷa caṭaṅku (itaṟku upasampatam eṉṟu peyar) niccayikkappaṭṭatu. āyiṉum saṅkatti ēkōpikkacceyta ēṟpāṭukaḷ kikūṭavilli.

puttarukkup piṉ avaruṭiya āpta cīṣarkaḷ ñāṉappokkiṣameṉṟu maṟṟōrāl karutappaṭuvatu iyalpē, ivarkaḷ kūṟiyavi puttariṉ cuya vacaṉaṅkaḷākakak karutappaṭṭu, intap piratama cīṣarkaḷiṉ cīṣarkaḷāl maṉappāṭañ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. intu matak kirantaṅkaḷip pōlavē puttap pirapantaṅkaḷum vāymoḻiyākak kuruciṣya parampariyil niliniṉṟu vantaṉa. āṉāl piratama cīṣarkaḷukkuḷḷēyē apippirāya pētaṅkaḷ iruntamipaṟṟi puttarukkuḷ piḷavukaḷ ēṟpaṭattoṭaṅkiṉa. kutamariṟanta nūṟu varuṣaṅkaḷukkup piṟaku koḷki vēṟupāṭukaḷum ācāra vēṟupāṭukaḷum uṇmiyāṉa matap pirapantaṅkaḷ eviyeṉṟa camucayamum mēliṭṭiruntaṉa. ikkuṟikaḷip parikārañceyya matat talivarkaḷ maṟṟoru makā sapi kūṭṭiṉar. inta iraṇṭāvatu putta makā sapi veycāliyil naṭantatu. saṅkattil ēṟpaṭṭirunta pala uṭpirivukaḷāl sapiyiṉ naṭavaṭikkikaḷ muṟṟuppeṟavilli. ataṉ vēli citintatu ; pala etir sapikaḷ kūṭiṉa ; pirapantaṅkaḷi niccayippatum tiraṭṭuvatum cāttiyamillāmal pōyiṉa. iraṇṭāvatu sapiyāl kuḻappam atikarittatallāmal vēṟu kuṇa muṇṭākavilli.

ākavē acōkaṉ ciṅkātaṉamēṟiya kālattil putta saṅkattil kuṟṟaṅkaḷ malintiruntaṉa. ācāraṅkaḷilum koḷkikaḷilum viyavasti irukkavilli. makap pirapantaṅkaḷ eviyeṉṟu tīrmāṉamākavilli. intiyāviṉ pala pākaṅkaḷilumirunta pikṣuk cuṭṭaṅkaḷ taṅkaḷukku ucitameṉṟu tōṉṟiya ceykikaḷiyum, cariyeṉṟu tōṉṟiya koḷkikaḷiyum kikkoṇṭiruntaṉar. pāṭimōkham eṉṟa caṭaṅku kūṭac cariyāka eṅkum naṭipeṟṟu varavilliyeṉṟu sārnāt cāsaṉattiliruntu nām ūkikkalām.

mūṉṟāvatu putta makā sapi acōkaṉuṭiya kaṭicikkālattil pāṭalipurattil kukkuṭārāmam eṉṟu ﻿

mūṉṟāvatu

caṅka capi

﻿peyar vaḻaṅkiya putta maṭattil mōkalī puttiratissaṉ eṉṟa ācāriyaṉiṉ talimiyiṉ kīḻ naṭipeṟṟatu. intiyāvil ellāp pākaṅkaḷiliruntum pikṣukkaḷ varavaḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. sapiyiṉ naṭavaṭikkikaḷ muṟṟuppeṟa oṉpatu mātaṅkaḷ ceṉṟaṉa. sapiyiṉ aṅkattiṉar ellōrum camam eṉṟa vitiyiṉ paṭi kāriyaṅkaḷ tīrmāṉikkappaṭṭaṉavātalāl it tīrmāṉaṅkaḷil acōkaṉuṭiya cuya apippirāyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu nām eṇṇa iṭamilli. āyiṉum, aracaṉum pikṣuvāṉatu paṟṟi sapiyiṉ naṭavaṭikkikaḷil avaṉ campantappaṭṭirukkalām.

inta sapiyil mika mukkiyamāṉa kāriyaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. tīya ācāraṅkaḷum tappāṉa koḷkikaḷum kaṇṭaṉañ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. matakkirantaṅkaḷ eviyeṉṟu tīrmāṉañ ceyyappaṭṭu tripiṭakam eṉṟu peyaruṭiya mūṉṟu puttap pirapantat tokutikaḷum iccamayam varicipaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. tappāṉa koḷkikaḷit tavirkkum poruṭṭu katāvastu eṉṟa kirantam sapit talivarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. tiraṭṭappaṭṭa kirantaṅkaḷ muṟṟilum pārāyaṇañ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ippuviyiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷukkum tarmattip pōtikkap pirācārarkaḷi aṉuppavēṇṭumeṉṟu tīrmāṉittu sapiyōr ataṟkavaciyamāṉa pōtakarkaḷiyum terinteṭuttaṉar.

sapiyiṉ tīrmāṉaṅkaḷi uṟuti ceyya aracaṉuṭiya celvākkut tāṅkutalāy iruntataṉāl immūṉṟāvatu makācapi muntiya capiyippōl caṇṭiyilum kuḻappattilum muṭiyavilli. sārnāt stampattiluḷḷa cāsaṉam, inta sapiyiṉ tīrmāṉatti amulukkuk koṇṭuvaravē eṉpatu teḷivu. sāñci stampattilum pirayāki stampattilum itē kaṭṭaḷi piracurañ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. āyiṉum inta mūṉṟāvatu makā sapiyiṉ naṭavaṭikkikaḷkūṭa puttariṭiyil sarvacammatamāyirukkavillipōlum. ēṉeṉṟāl inta sapi kūṭiya viṣayamēṉum vaṭa intiya aitihyattil kiṭiyātu. itiliruntu mikap pirapalamāṉa apippirāya pētaṅkaḷ puttarukkuḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟu nām ūkikkalām. itu hīṉa yāṉam mahā yāṉam eṉṟa iru perum matappirivukaḷiṉ toṭakkattic cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.

mahāyāṉam, hīṉayāṉam eṉṟa coṟkaḷ muṟiyē peruṅkappal eṉṟum ciṟukappal eṉṟum poruḷpaṭum. mahāyāṉa matattār tamatu matatti, piṟavikkaṭalik kaṭappataṟku muṟṟilum nampak kūṭiya perumarakkalattōṭu oppiṭṭu, etirk kakṣiyāriṉ camayatti, vēkamāyccelvatum āṉāl apāyakaramāṉatumāṉa ciṟu paṭakōṭu oppiṭṭatiliruntu ic coṟkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa.

ellā mataṅkaḷum, jīvaṉ, pirakiruti, īcuvaraṉ, puṉar ﻿

saṉmārkka

pōtaṉiyē

tipeḷatta matat

tiṉ cārameṉal

﻿jaṉmam, karmapantam, mōkṣam eṉṟa viṣayaṅkaḷippaṟṟip pirattiyēka apippirāyaṅkaḷuṭiyaṉa. acōkaṉuṭiya cāsaṉaṅkaḷil avaṉ ivaṟṟippaṟṟi oṉṟumē collavilli. acōkaṉuṭiya apippirāyappaṭi tarmam eṉpatu ippaṭippaṭṭa matakkoḷkikaḷaṉṟu, “ellōriyum kāppāṟṟakkūṭiyatu etuvō atuvē tarmam”, eṉṟa accolliṉ uṭkaruttē tarmattiṉ lakṣaṇameṉṟu acōkaṉ uṇarntāṉ.avaṉ koḷkippaṭi puttapakavāṉiṉ makimi eṉṉaveṉṟāl, ellā maṉitarukkum uyar naṉṉeṟiyi vaḻipaṭutal avaciyam eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiyatē. naṉṉaṭakkiyum nalleṇṇaṅkaḷumē kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu, maṟṟa mataṅkaḷuṭiyavum sārāmcam saṉmārkkapōtaṉi tāṉeṉṟu acōkaṉ eṇṇiṉatāl mataccaṇṭikaḷiṉ iḻiviyum antiyamataṅkaḷit tūṣippatiṉ keṭutaliyum avaṉ naṉkuṇarntāṉ.

acōkaṉ tarmam eṉṟa colliṉāl eṉṉa karuttik kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉeṉṟu aintu āṟu cāsaṉaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. tāy tantiyarukku veku matippu, vayōtikar ñāṉacirēṣṭarkaḷukku mariyāti, ēḻikaḷukkum pirāmaṇar camaṇar mutalāṉōrukkum īki, aṭimikaḷ cēvakarkaḷiṭam aṉpu, jīva jantukkaḷiṭattil irakkam, ahimsi , atāvatu cakala jīvarācikaḷiṭattum aṉutāpam, pulāl uṇṇāmi, vāymi, piṟar mataṅkaḷit tūṣaṇiceyyāmi, camarasapāvam, ivitāṉ tarmameṉṟu acōkaṉ vaṟpuṟuttukiṟāṉ.

āyiṉum acōkaṉiṉ matam cila viṣayaṅkaḷil ﻿

mūṭanampikki

kaḷik

kaṇṭittal

﻿pāmara jaṉaṅkaḷuṭiya koḷkikaḷōṭu muraṇpaṭṭatu. “maṉitaṉiṉ maṉappōkkum iccikaḷum palavitamāka vēṟupaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa: cila matastar eṉcāsaṉikaḷi muṟṟilum piṉpaṟṟalām, maṟṟa vakuppār eṉcāsaṉikaḷiṉ cila pākaṅkaḷip piṉpaṟṟalām.“ eṉṟu ēḻām cāsaṉattilum, “stirījaṉaṅkaḷ iḻivum arttamaṟṟatum niṣpirayōjaṉamumāṉa nāṉāvita cillaṟic caṭaṅkukaḷic ceykiṟārkaḷ.” eṉṟu eṭṭām cāsaṉattilum kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiliruntu maṟṟamataṅkaḷ evvaḷavu tūram saṉmārkka pōtaṉiyi aṉusarikkiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavē avi uṇmiyeṉṟum, stirī jaṉaṅkaḷ vīṭukaḷil ceyyum mantira karumaṅkaḷ iḻiveṉṟum aracaṉ matittatāka namakkut teriyavarukiṟatu. āṉāl acōkaṉ inta apippirāyaṅkaḷi aṭikkaṭi eṭutturittu jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ maṉattip puṇpaṭuttavilli. atumaṭṭumaṉṟu, avaṉ ellā mataṅkaḷiccērnta tuṟavikaḷukkum ñāṉikaḷukkum pūji mariyātikaḷ ceytāṉ.

acōkaṉuṭiya nōkkaṅkaḷ nāḷaṭivil virintu ﻿

jīva imci

nivāraṇam

﻿koṇṭē vantaṉaveṉpatum muṉ colliya vivaraṅkaḷiliruntu nām ūkikkalām. jīvavatiyippaṟṟi aracaṉ koṇṭirunta apippirāyaṅkaḷum māṟutal aṭintaṉaveṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. aracaṉiṉ āḷukiyiṉ toṭakkattil jīvavati paliyiṭutal mutaliya vaḻakkaṅkaḷ muṟṟilum tavirkkappaṭṭaṉapōlum, tāṉ amitamāṉa uṟcākattuṭaṉ ivvitam piravarttittatākap piṉ uṇarntu irupattēḻāvatu paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattil aracaṉ jīva vatiyik kuṟippataṟkum aṉāvaciyamāṉa jantu imsiyi nivāraṇañ ceyvataṟkum maṭṭumē ettaṉittāṉ.

cāsaṉaṅkaḷil, maṉitaṉ cuvarkkatti aṭiya ﻿

cuvarkkattil

nampikki

﻿vēṇṭumeṉṟum, evarukkum immi yil ceyyavēṇṭiya kaṭamiyi nampikki niṟivēṟṟuvatiṉ payaṉ maṟumiyil cuvarkkacukameṉṟum tirumpat tirumpak kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu viyappāyirukkiṟatu. puttar pōtitta catur ārya sattiyamum, eṭṭuvakittiyāṉaṅkaḷum maṉita vāḻviṉ muṭivākiya nirvāṇamum acōkaṉāl maṟakkappaṭṭaṉavā ? illi. āṉāl kālakkiramattil putta matattil uṇṭāṉa māṟutalkaḷukku acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷ aṟikuṟiyāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. acōkaṉ taṉatu pirajikaḷukku naṟkuṇa naṟceykikaḷiṉ avaciyatti vaṟpuṟatta eṇṇiyatāl avvaḻiyil naṭappatiṉ payaṉāṉa cuvarkkattip paṟṟiyum avaṉukku eṭutturippatu avaciyamāyiṟṟu. cuvarkkameṉpatu tāṉ pāmara jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ piravirttikaḷukku ēṟṟa tūṇṭutal eṉṟu acōkaṉ uṇarntāṉpōlum. kuṭalyaṉuṭiya arttacāstirattilum ikkoḷki vicēṣamākac collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.

kutama puttarum cuvarkkāṉupavattip paṟṟik kūṟuvatu tappitamāka niṉikkavilli. āṉāl avaruṭiya mukkiyamāṉa uttēcam, āci mōkam mutaliya kaṭṭukkaḷi aṟuttut taḷḷakkūṭiya palavāṉkaḷi oṉṟu cērttut tiraṭṭi avari oru caṅkamākac cērkka vēṇṭumeṉpatu, ataṟkiṇaṅka avar vekuvākap piṟappu, iṟappu, mūppu, piṇi eṉṟa tukkaṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ koṭumiyiyum, avaṟṟiliruntu viṭupaṭuvataṟku ēṟṟa nivirttimārkkattiyum paṟṟip pōtittār. āṉāl avar cātāraṇa jaṉaṅkaḷiṭam iv viṣayaṅkaḷippaṟṟip pēcāmal aṉpu, vāymi, īki eṉṟa kuṇaṅkaḷiṉ mēṉmiyippaṟṟip pēciṉār.

puttar kālattukkup piṉ avar ciṣyarkaḷ piravirtti nivirtti eṉṟa iruvitamārkkaṅkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumiyip poruṭpaṭuttāmal tarmam eṉpatu ﻿

acōkaṉuk

kku piṉ put

ta matattil ēṟ

paṭṭamāṟutalkaḷ

﻿nirvāṇattiyaṭiyum vaḻiyeṉṟu stāpittaṉar. ataṉāl putta saṅkattil illaṟattārukku iṭamillāmaṟ pōyiṟṟu. acōkaṉ ikkuṟiyi uṇarntāṉpōlum. ataṉāl avaṉ tarmam eṉpatu saṉmārkkavaḻiyē yaṉṟi vēṟilliyeṉṟu muṟiyiṭṭāṉ. āyiṉum avaṉ illaṟattārukkup puttar eṉṟa peyarkoṭuttu avari oru pirattiyēka camūkattārākac ceyyavilli, taṟkālam kiṟistu mukammatiya mataṅkaḷippōla puttamatam pala kuṭumpattārukkup piṟappu iṟappu kaliyāṇam mutaliya caṭaṅkukaḷi naṭattivikkap purōkitarkaḷit tayār ceyyavilli. ataṉāl nāḷaṭivil pattaruṭiya laṉmārkka pōtaṉi maṭṭum intiyāviṉ jaṉaṅkaḷuṭiya vāḻvil ūḷḷūṟapparavi yataṉuṭaṉ kalantu pōyiṟṟu.

intiyāviṉ puṟattil puttaruṭiya matam vēṟu vitam vikasittatu. tipet cīṉā jappāṉ eṉṟa nāṭukaḷil puttamatam nirīcvaravātam aṉātmavātam mutaliya koḷkikaḷi iḻantu kaṭavuḷiṭam nampikkiyuḷḷa matamāy māṟiṟṟu. pattaṉ eṉṟa patam muṉ oru kālattilirunta ñāṉiyiṉ peyarāyirāmal yāvarum tamatu muyaṟciyāl aṭiyakkūṭiya āṉmīya nilimiyiṉ peyarāka cērntatu. kutama puttarukku muṉṉum piṉṉum anēkar puttarāyirukkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟa koḷki paraviṟṟu. pūrvaputtar irunta viṣayatti acōkaṉum oppukkoṇṭāṉ eṉpatu muṉṉē coṉṉōm. ippaṭi, paṭippaṭiyāka mahāyāṉam eṉṟa puttamatapētam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. puttaṉ eṉṟa patam kaṭavuḷiṉ vēṟu oru peyarākavum pariṇamittatu. cilōṉ tīvilum parmāvilum taṟkālam paraviyirukkum puttamatattukku hīṉayāṉam eṉṟu peyar. hīṉayāṉamatattār maṉōtattuvaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyavariyilum pirācīṉa puttak koḷkikaḷiyē pōtittu varukiṉṟaṉar.





IV. acōkaṉ aracāṭci



nām muriya ēkātipattiyattiṉ āṭcimuṟiyip ﻿

acōkaṉ

turittaṉam

muṉṉiruntataṉ

toṭarcciyē

﻿paṟṟi virivāka eḻutakkūṭum. cantira kuptaṉuṭiya kālattil avaṉuṭiya rājatāṉiyil pala varuṣaṅkaḷāka vacittuvanta yavaṉa tūtaṉ mekāstaṉis taṉ cutēcam ceṉṟatum tāṉ kaṇṭaṉavum kēṭṭaṉavumāṉa ceytikaḷittiraṭṭi, intiya vivaraṇam, eṉṟa pustakatti amittāṉ. itaṉ cila pākaṅkaḷē ippōtu, namakku akappaṭukiṉṟaṉa ; āyiṉum, mōriya aracāṭciyiṉ taṉmi, intiya jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ oḻukka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ nāṭṭiṉ nīr nilavaḷam mutaliya pala viṣayaṅkaḷi nām avaṟṟiliruntu uṇarntu koḷḷalām. kuṭalyaṉ, cāṇakkiyaṉ, viṣṇukuptaṉ eṉṟu palavāṟu peyar kūṟappaṭum rājatantira nipuṇaṉ cantira kuptaṉuṭiya mantiriyāyiruntāṉeṉṟum, avaṉatu cakāyattālēyē cantirakuptaṉ ciṅkātaṉattik kippaṟṟiṉāṉeṉṟum, kuṭalyaṉāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭateṉṟu matikkappaṭum arttacāstiram eṉṟa oru nūl uṇṭeṉṟum nām muṉṉē kūṟiṉōm. piṟkālaṅkaḷil innūl tarma virōtameṉṟu karutappaṭṭatāl vitvāṉkaḷ itaṉ ārāycciyip pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka acaṭṭiceytaṉar. āyiṉum cilavaruṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ tañcāvūr jillāviliruntu ippustakattiṉ pirati kiṭittirukkiṉṟatu. ippirati kiṭittatu mutal inta arttacāstirattiṉ āciriyaṉ, kālam, itaṉ mutal nūlkaḷ, itiluḷḷa viṣayaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa palavita ārāyccikaḷukkuk kāraṇamāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. kuṭalyaṉuṭiya arttacāstirattiliruntu teriyavarum vivaraṅkaḷi ēṟakkuṟiya cantirakuptaṉuṭiya ēkātipattiyattiṉ taṉmiyākak koḷḷalāmeṉṟu aṟiñar oppukkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.

mekāstaṉis eḻutiyuḷḷa pustakattiṉ pākaṅkaḷiyum kuṭalyaṉuṭiy arttacāstirattiyum icittu nōkkumiṭattu mōriya aracāṭci, toṭakkattil evvitam naṭipeṟṟatu eṉpatu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. pūrva intiya carittirattil vēṟu ekkālattiṟkum ittakiya nuṭpamāṉa vivaraṇam kiṭippatilli. atu maṭṭumalla, itaṟku oppāṉa vivaraṇattik kāṇpataṟku nām ki. pi. patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭuvariyum pirayāṇañ ceyyavēṇṭum. akpar kālattatāṉa ‘āyiṉ-i-akparī’, eṉṟa turittaṉaviḷakkattil maṭṭumē nām ittakiya vivaraṅkaḷik kāṇalām.

āyiṉum cantirakuptaṉuṭiya turittaṉatti vivarippatu ivviyācattiṟkup puṟampāṉatu. acōkaṉuṭiya putitāṉa rājīya nōkkaṅkaḷi viḷakkuvataṟku evvaḷavu vēṇṭumō avvaḷavē ivaṟṟippaṟṟi iṅkuk kūṟuvatu cāttiyam. acōkaṉuṭiya kālattil ēkātipattiyattiṉ perumi oruvitattilum kuṉṟavilli. rāṇuva palattilum, nirvāka atikārikaḷiṉ tiṟamiyilum, tēcattil vacittuvanta anniyanāṭṭāri ātarittup pōṟṟum nākarikattilum, viyāpāra apivirttiyilum, kittoḻiṟperukkattilum acōkaṉatu āḷuki muṉṉiruntati viṭak koñcamuṅ kuṟivaṭiyavilli. pāṭalipurattic cuṟṟiya mattiyappiratēcattit tavirttu maṟṟap pākaṅkaḷ nāṉku mākāṇaṅkaḷāyp pirikkappaṭṭu iḷavaracar palarāl kāppāṟṟappaṭṭuvantaṉa. acōkaṉuṭiya kālattil immākāṇaṅkaḷāvaṉa -1. takṣacilā allatu takkaciliyit talinakarākavuṭiya vaṭamēṟku mākāṇam. 2. ujjeyiṉiyit talinakaramākavuṭiya māḷavatēcam 3. tosāliyit talinakaramākakkoṇṭa kaliṅkam. 4. cuvarṇakiriyit talinakarākakkoṇṭa takkaṇamākāṇam. immākāṇaṅkaḷil kāriya nirvākattiṟkup pala mantirikaḷum paṭippaṭiyākap palavita atikārikaḷum iruntaṉar. mekāstaṉis colliyiruppatupōla, mukkiyamāṉa nakaraṅkaḷilum rāṇuvattilum aintumemparkaḷ aṭaṅkiya kamiṭṭiyār vēli ceytu vantaṉar. ēkātipattiyattiṉ turittaṉamāṉatu pala cakkaraṅkaḷiyuṭiya periya iyantiratti yottiruntatu.

acōkaṉuṭiya turittaṉam mēṟkūṟiyapaṭi pala ﻿

aracaṉatu putu

nōkkaṅkaḷ

﻿vitattil, muṉṉē rājyattil ēṟpaṭṭirunta turittaṉattiṉ toṭarcciyāyiruntapōtilum araciyalil avaṉukkup putiya nōkkaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avaṉ turittaṉam eṉṟa iyantirattil aṉāvaciyamāykkiyiṭṭuk kaṣṭaṅkaḷil cikkikkoḷḷavilli yāyiṉum turittaṉam eṉpatu uyir uṇarvu, kaṇṇōṭṭam mutaliya māṉiṭakuṇaṅkaḷiṉ valikkuṭpaṭāta veṟum iyantiramāyiruppatu naṉṟalla veṉṟu karutiṉāṉ. avaṉuṭiya cuyavākkiyaṅkaḷil iruntē aracaṉ nōkkaṅkaḷi nām tīrmāṉikkalām. ‘tarmattiṉālētāṉ paripālaṉam uṇṭu ; tarmattiṉālē tāṉ oḻuṅkuṇṭu; tarmattiṉālē tāṉ cukkiyamaṭiyalām’ (mutal stampacāsaṉam). taṉatu araciyal tarmam eṉṟa aṭivārattiṉmēl kaṭṭappaṭavēṇṭumeṉṟu avaṉ ācippaṭṭatāka ivvācakam namakku aṟivikkiṉṟatu. ‘ellā maṉitarum eṉatu makkaḷ; eṉatu kuḻantikuṭṭikaḷukku immiyilum maṟumiyilum ellāvita cantōṣa cupākkiyaṅkaḷum uṇṭākavēṇṭumeṉṟu nāṉ ācippaṭuvatu pōlavē ellā maṉitarukkum aṉukkirakaṅkaḷik virumpukiṟēṉ” “ evarukkum eṉṉippaṟṟik koñcamum accamvēṇṭām ; niccayamāy eṉṉāl avarkaḷukku viyacaṉam uṇṭākātu. cantōṣam maṭṭumē uṇṭākum. aracaṉ etiyum kūṭumāṉavariyil kṣamiyuṭaṉ poṟuttukkoḷḷum cupāvamuṭiyavaṉ” (kaliṅka cāsaṉam). ivvākkiyaṅkaḷum acōkaṉuṭiya āṭciyiṉ taṉmiyi viḷakka eṟṟatāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. tiṉamum ākāyatti nōkki vacittuvarum nam nāṭṭu jaṉaṅkaḷippōla maṉṉavaṉ kōli muṟṟilum nampi akkālattukkuṭikaḷum vāḻntu vantaṉar. acōkaṉ makimi eṉṉaveṉṟāl, avaṉ kuṭikaḷiṉ kṣēmattil taṉakku ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa poṟuppi naṉku uṇarntāṉ eṉpatē.

niṉitta kāriyaṅkaḷil jayam oṉṟē aracar kavaṉikka vēṇṭuvatu, poymiyum cūtum pērāciyum araciyalukku iyalpu eṉpatu kuṭalyaṉuṭiya koḷkipōlum. avaṉ aracarkaḷi tēvarkaḷukkuc camāṉamāka uyartti avarkaḷukkuc cātāraṇa maṉitarkkuriya tarmaniyāyaṅkaḷ amiyā eṉṟu urittāṉ. acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷiluḷḷa pala vākkiyaṅkaḷ ikkoḷkiyi maṟutturippataṟku eḻutappaṭṭavi pōl olikkiṉṟaṉa. jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ naṭattikku mātiriyāka vāḻavēṇṭuvatu aracaṉ kaṭami eṉṟu pala iṭaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa (utāraṇam ; iraṇṭām stampa cāsaṉam, ēḻām stampa cāsaṉam.) “muṉṉē maṉitar tēvarkaḷi aṉucarittu oḻukavilli, ippoḻutu jampūt tīpattil maṉitar tēvarkaḷi aṉucarittu oḻukukiṉṟaṉar. itu eṉṉuṭiya uḻippiṉ payaṉ...... ciṟiyōrum periyōrum (nalvaḻiyil) uḻittu varavēṇṭum" eṉṟu acōkaṉ taṉatu pirajikaḷukku vaṟpuṟuttukiṟāṉ (mutal upacāsaṉam).

patiṉāṉku mukkiya cāsaṉaṅkaḷil paṉṉiraṇṭām cāsaṉam ﻿

camarasapāvam

﻿pāvattiṉ lakṣaṇattiyum ataṉ avaciyattiyum ataṟku virōtamāṉa maṉappāṅkiyum naṉṟāka viḷakkukiṟatu. ellāc camayaṅkaḷilum uyarnta koḷkikaḷum uyarnaṉṉeṟiyil oḻukum māṉiṭarkaḷumuṇṭeṉṟu maṟṟac cāsaṉaṅkaḷilum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu, paṉṉiraṇṭām cāsaṉam poṉṉeḻuttiṟ poṟikkum takimittu. acōkaṉ ellā mataṅkaḷiyum vekumatittu, pala matattiṉariyum oṉṟupōl kāppāṟṟivantāṉ. jaṉaṅkaḷiṭiyil paraspara ciṉēkamum oṟṟumiyum vaḷarntuvantaṉa. taṉ ayalāṉ emmatattāṉāyiṉumcari avaṉuṭaṉ kūṭivāḻalām eṉṉum uṇmiyum, matakkoḷkikaḷ paṟṟi oruvaṉuṭiya naṉṉaṭakki pōyviṭāteṉṟa uṇmiyum, palakālamvariyum airōppāviluḷḷa jaṉaṅkaḷāl uṇarappaṭavilli. vāḷ muṉiyikkāṭṭi maṉitarip payamuṟutti matakkoḷkikaḷ piracāram ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. koḷki vēṟupāṭukaḷukkākap palar uyiruṭaṉ neruppiliṭappaṭṭu pocukkappaṭṭaṉar. nāṭṭil orēvitamāṉa matakkoḷkikaḷē naṭamāṭavēṇṭumeṉṟa āci pala aracariyum mata kurumārkaḷiyum aniyāyac ceykikaḷukkum akkiramaṅkaḷukkum tūṇṭukōlāyiruntirukkiṟatu. ataṉāl, iraṇṭārayiram varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉirunta ōr intiya aracaṉ, jaṉaṅkaḷ matakkoḷkikaḷukkākac caṇṭi pōṭṭuk koḷḷakkūṭāteṉṟum, nāṉāvitamatattiṉar pāspara veku matippuṭaṉum aṉpuṭaṉum aṭuttaṭuttu vāḻntu varalāmeṉṟum, ellāmataṅkaḷilum cirēṣṭamāṉa tattuvaṅkaḷ uṇṭeṉṟum kūṟiyiruppatu āccariyamallavā? mataṅkaḷ viṣayattil acōkaṉ kāṭṭiya camatiruṣṭi rājatantirattiṉ amcameṉṟu nām niṉikka iṭamilli. itu acōkaṉuṭiya uyarnta maṉōpāvattiyē kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu. akkālattu intiya jaṉaṅkaḷum samayaccaṇṭikaḷikkoḷḷātu camarasapāvatti ātarittaṉar eṉalām. “ōr jātiyāriṉ nākarikatti matippataṟku avar viññāṉacāstiraṅkaḷil aṭintirukkum tērcciyum nīr neruppu kāṟṟu mutaliya iyaṟkic caktikaḷi aṭakkiyāḷum tiṟamiyum alla aḷavukaruvikaḷ, camasarapāvattiṉ mutircciyē cariyāṉa aḷavukaruvi.” eṉṟu ōr carittira āciriyar kūṟukiṟār. ivvapippirāyam cariyeṉiṉ, irunūṟu varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉkūṭa airōppiya tēcaṅkaḷil ēṟpaṭāta uyarnta nākarikatti ki. mu mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē nam tēcattu jaṉaṅkaḷ aṭintiruntaṉar.

rājyattiṟ cupikṣattipperukkavum jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ ﻿

kṣēmāpi

virttikkāṉa

ēṟpāṭukaḷ

﻿kṣēmattiṉ poruṭṭum pala ēṟpāṭukaḷ acōkaṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ kūṟiyuḷḷa vākkiyaṅkaḷālēyē ivaṟṟi nām urikkalām, “tēvar piriyaṉāṉa piyatasi rājaṉāl āḷappaṭṭa ellāp pākaṅkaḷilum ........ atumaṭṭumaṉṟu ...... ayal aracar nāṭṭilum ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum aracaṉ cikitsikkāka iruvita ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceytirukkiṟāṉ. aḵtāvatu, maṉitarukku vittiyacāli, mirukaṅkaḷukku vittiyacāli eṉpaṉavē. mēlum, maṉitarukku upayōkamāṉatum mirukaṅkaḷukku upayōkamāṉatumāṉa maruntu mūlikikaḷ eviyō, avi kiṭikkum iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu kiṭikkā iṭaṅkaḷukku koṇṭuvarappaṭṭup payirākkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, kaṉi, kāy kiḻaṅkukaḷum varavaḻikkappaṭṭup payir ceyyap paṭukiṉṟaṉa........... pātikaḷil kiṇaṟukaḷ veṭṭavum niḻal tarum maraṅkaḷ vaḷarkkavum ēṟpāṭu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa” (iraṇṭām cāsaṉam). “nāṉ māntōppukkaḷi vaḷarkkappaṇṇiyirukkiṟēṉ. arikkurōcattukku oru taṭavi kēṇikaḷ veṭṭic cāvaṭikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. mirukaṅkaḷukkum maṉitarukkum cukattik koṭukkum poruṭṭu nāṉ pala taṇṇīrp pantalkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttiyirukkiṟēṉ” (ēḻām stampacāsaṉam). ivvita ēṟpāṭukaḷ acōkaṉukku muṉṉirunta aracarkaḷālum ceyyappaṭṭirukkalāmāyiṉum ivvivaram acōkaṉ pukaḻik kuṟikkātu .



acōkaṉāl jaṉaṅkaḷukku niyāyam kiṭikkumeṉṟu uṟutimoḻi kūṟappaṭṭatu.

“allalpaṭṭu āṟṟātu aḻutakaṇ ṇīr aṉṟē

celvattit tēykkum paṭi” - (56-5)





eṉṟa tiruvaḷḷuvar vacaṉattiṉ uṇmiyi acōkaṉ uṇarntāṉ pōlum. aniyāyamāṉa, cikṣi, ciṟiceytal mutaliya kuṟṟaṅkaḷ aracāṭci ceyvōrukku iyalpē. ivvita kuṟṟaṅkaḷi nivirtticeyyum. kaṭamiyi acōkaṉ tāṉ putitāka niyamitta tarma makāmāttirar eṉṟa aticārikaḷiṭam oppittāṉ : rājyattil vēlipārttu vanta palavita atikārikaḷuṭiya kaṭamiyil kavaṉamiṉmi allatu kaḷḷam nērumāyiṉ atu cakkaravarttikkuc ceyyappaṭum perunturōkameṉṟu avarkaḷ eccarikki ceyyappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ tam nilimi tavaṟi naṭakkiṟārkaḷāveṉṟu terintu koḷḷa rakaciyamāka oṟṟarkaḷ palar aṉuppappaṭṭaṉar. cakkaravartti jaṉaṅkaḷukku aḷitta vākkuttattaṅkaḷum piratikñikaḷum aṭikkaṭi urakkap paṭikkappaṭṭaṉa ; kaṟkaḷil eḻuti viḷamparam ceyyappaṭṭaṉa (kaliṅkacāsaṉam). “nakara atikārikaḷ akāraṇamāy maṉitaric ciṟiceytal imcittal mutaliya tīya vaḻakkaṅkaḷit tavirppataṟkāka iccāsaṉam variyappaṭṭatu”. ippaṭikku oru cāsaṉattiṉ muṭivil varintiruppatum ivviṭattil kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

taṟkālattilum muṟkālaṅkaḷilum maṇṇi uḻutu

﻿

vivacāyam

﻿payiriṭṭu jīvaṉañ ceyvōrē tēcattiṉ kṣēmattiṟku astivāram pōṉṟavar. acōkaṉ vēḷāṇmakkaḷiṉ vēṇṭutalkaḷi maṟakkavilli yeṉpataṟku avaṉuṭiya moḻiyiliruntē namakku ātāram kiṭikkavilliyāyiṉum vēṟoruvaḻiyāka veku iṉpakaramāṉa ātāram akappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. kattiyavāṭ allatu kujarāt eṉappaṭum tīpakaṟpattiṉ naṭuvil kirnār eṉṟa oru mali uṇṭu, aṅkuḷḷa pāṟiyil acōkaṉuṭiya patiṉāṉku cilācāsaṉaṅkaḷum eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. muṉ appākatti āṇṭuvanta vēṟu pala aracariṉ likitaṅkaḷum aṅkirukkiṉṟaṉa. ki.pi. 150-m ௵l aṅkē aracāṭci ceyta rutratāmaṉ eṉṟa kṣatrapa aracaṉ eḻutiyuḷḷa likitattil acōkaṉatu peyarum avaṉ vivacāyattiṟkākacceyta vēliyoṉṟum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. cutarcaṉa ēri eṉṟa periya ēri oṉṟu pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka iruntu vantatu. rutratāmaṉ kālattil avvēri pala vitamāṉa cīrkēṭuṟṟu vivacāyikaḷukku utavātiruntatu. avaṉ cutarcaṉa ēriyic cīrtirutti ataṟkup putiya kālvāykaḷum cīppukkaḷum amitta vivaraṅkaḷē avaṉuṭiya kalveṭṭiṉ viṣayam. āyiṉum anta likitattil rutratāmaṉ cutarcaṉa ēriyiṉ pūrva carittirattiyum kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ, atu cantirakuptaṉ kālattil mutalil veṭṭappaṭṭateṉṟum, āṉāl - appōtu jalattip pāycca avaciyamāṉa kālvāykaḷ yāvum pūrttiyāka veṭṭappaṭavilliyeṉṟum, acōkaṉ kālvāykaḷi veṭṭic cila cīppukaḷiyum amittāṉeṉṟum iṅkuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. acōkaṉ ippiratēcattil tuṣāspaṉ eṉṟa yavaṉa aracaṉit taṉatu piratinitiyāka vittu ikkāriyaṅkaḷic ceytu muṭittāṉām.[1]

acōkaṉāl stāpikkappaṭṭa ālayaṅkaḷum maṭaṅkaḷum ﻿

kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ

﻿ki. pi nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāhiyaṉ eṉṟa cīṉa yāttirikaṉ iṅku vanta kālattilēyē eṇṇiṟantaṉaveṉṟu karutappaṭṭaṉa. putta matattārukku maṭṭumalla ellōrukkum pirayōjaṉap paṭukiṉṟa pala kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum stāpaṉaṅkaḷum acōkaṉāṟ ceyyap paṭṭaṉa, pāṭalipurattil ēṟakkuṟiya āyiram pikṣukkaḷukku aṉṉa vastiraṅkaḷ aḷittu avarik kāppāṟṟi vanta acōkārāmam eṉṟa putta maṭam ēṟpaṭṭiruntatu. ataṉ arukiluḷḷa nālantā eṉṟa ūr pala cāstiraṅkaḷum kali ñāṉaṅkaḷum taḻitta periya vittiyāpīṭamāy viḷaṅkiṟṟu, takṣaciliyiṉ kalvip pukaḻum kuṉṟātu perukiyatu. jaṉaṅkaḷukku iṭiyil eḻuti vācikkak kūṭiya tiṟami apūrvamāyirukkavilli yeṉpataṟku acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷē cāṉṟākum.

acōkacāsaṉaṅkaḷiṟ palavaki atikārikaḷiṉ ﻿

cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṟ

kūṟappaṭum

atikārikaḷ

﻿peyar kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. atikārikaḷiṉ peyar evvitaṅkaḷil varukiṉṟatu eṉpati ōr paṭṭikiyil eḻuti viḷakkalām.





* * *



cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum⁠

rāja atikārikaḷ,  ivarkaḷik kuṟippiṭum cāsaṉaṅkaḷ,



* * *





1. tarmamakāmāttirar.⁠  5-m cāsaṉam 7-m stampacāsaṉam.

2. rajūkar⁠  3-m cā. 4-m stampacā.

3. prātēcikar⁠  3-m cāsaṉam.

4. makāmāttirar.⁠  sārnāt cā. irāṇikāruvākiyiṉ

⁠  likitam. kaliṅka cāsaṉaṅkaḷ.

⁠  6-m cāsaṉam. mutal upa cā.

⁠  7-m stampacāsaṉam.

5. antamakāmāttirar⁠  mutal stampacāsaṉam

6. pulisar⁠  1, 4, 7-m stampacāsaṉaṅkaḷ

7. virucapūmikar  12-m cā.

8. stirī makāmāttirar.  12-m cā.

8. tūtar.  13-m cāsaṉam.

8. yuktar.  3-m cā.

8. āyuktar.  iraṇṭām kaliṅka cā.

8. viyōhālakar.  mutal kaliṅka cā..

8. vyūtar.  mutal upacā, 7 m stampacā

8. pativētakar  6-m cāsaṉam.



* * *



ivvatikārikaḷuṭiya stāṉaṅkaḷiyum vēlikaḷiyum paṟṟi, cāsaṉaṅkaḷiliruntu maṭṭum nām aṟintu koḷvaṉa cilavē. kuṭalyaṉuṭiya arttacāstirattiyum akkālattatāṉa vēṟu nūlkaḷiyum āḻntu ārāyvōmeṉil ivviṣayam iṉṉum naṉku viḷaṅkalām,

acōkaṉ muṭicūṭiya patiṉāṉkāvatu paṭṭapiṣēka varuṣattil tarmamakāmāttirar eṉṟa putu uttiyōkam ciruṣṭikkappaṭṭatu (aintām stampa cāsaṉam). ivarkaḷ vēli tarmattip piracāram ceyvatum kāppāṟṟuvatum jaṉaṅkaḷukku upakāram ceyvatum tāṉa tarmaṅkaḷi naṭatti vippatumē. ivvatikārikaḷukkut taṉip piratēcam koṭukkappaṭavilliyeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. eppōtum pirayāṇañ ceytu, sarva viyāpakamāyiruntu, ellōrukkum naṉmikaḷic ceytu, aṉātikaḷukku aṉṉavastiraṅkaḷ aḷittuk kāppāṟṟi kāṭṭujātiyārukkum kuṟavarip pōla alintu tirintu vāḻkiṉṟa jaṉaṅkaḷukkum tarmattip pōtittu vara vēṇṭuvatē tarmamakāmāttirariṉ aluval eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.

rajūkar eṉpavar mākāṇaṅkaḷiṉ talivarāyiruntaṉar. arttacāstirattil ivarkaḷi stāṉikar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ivarkaḷiṉ āḷukiyil aṭaṅkiya piratēcattiṟkuc cāsaṉaṅkaḷil “jaṉapatam“ eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉ jaṉattoki aṉēkalakṣam eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. jaṉapatattiṉ turittaṉattil rajūkarukku muṉṉilum atika cutantiratti acōkaṉ aḷittatāka nāṉkām stampacā saṉam kūṟukiṉṟatu. prātēcikar eṉpavar rajūkariṉ kīḻuḷḷa nirvāka atikārikaḷām. makāmāttirar eṉṟa patam ivviruvaki atikārikaḷukkum maṟṟum palarukkum poruntum potup peyar. pulisar allatu puruṣar eṉappaṭupavar aracaṉ kāriya taricikaḷ pōlum. maṟṟa atikārikaḷiṉ vēliyi mēlnōkki avaric cōtaṉi ceyvatu ivar vēliyeṉṟu nām ūkikkalām. antamakāmāttirar eṉṟa atikārikaḷ kāṭṭujātiyāriyum ellippiratēcaṅkaḷiluḷḷa anākarika jaṉaṅkaḷiyum kavaṉittu vantaṉar. aracaṉuṭiya poṟumiyic cōtikkak kūṭiya tontaravukaḷ inta jaṉaṅkaḷiṭamiruntu vantu koṇṭiruntaṉaveṉṟu patiṉ mūṉṟām cilācāsaṉattil kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. pativētakar eṉpavar aracaṉuṭiya araṇmaṉiyil vicāraṇikkāka varum vaḻakkukkaḷik kavaṉikkavēṇṭiya atikārikaḷām. viṣayatti uṭaṉuṭaṉ aracaṉukkut terivittu niyāya vicāraṇiyi naṭatti, vaḻakkukaḷittīrppucceyvatu ivarkaḷuṭiya aluval eṉalām.

virucapūmikar eṉṟum stirīmakāmāttirar eṉṟum peyarpeṟṟa atikārikaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭām cāsaṉattiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl peyar kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟa cantarppattiliruntu avarkaḷuṭiya aluval ūkikkak kūṭavilli. viyōhālakar eṉpavar nakara atikārikaḷ pōlum, yuktar āyuktar eṉpavar kīḻttara atikārikaḷ. vyūtar eṉpavar tuṟavikaḷākiya tarmap piracārarkaḷ pōlum. cāsaṉaṅkaḷil varukiṉṟa coṟkaḷuḷ vyūtar eṉṟa patattiviṭa atikamākap paṇṭitarkaḷukku iṭiyil palavita vātappirativātaṅkaḷukkuk kāraṇamāyirukkum patam vēṟu illiyeṉṟu collalām.

aṉusamyāṉam eṉṟa rājīya stāpaṉattippaṟṟi ﻿

aṉusamyāṉam

﻿mūṉṟām cāsaṉattilum kaliṅka cāsaṉattilum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu, itaṉ karutteṉṉaveṉpati vitvāṉkaḷ iṉṉum niccayittuk kūṟavilli. atikārikaḷ stala māṟutal, cuṟṟuppirayāṇam ceytuvarutal eṉṟa poruḷ iṅkup poruttamuḷḷatāka namakkut tōṉṟavilli. ovvoru piratēcattiṉuṭiyavum mukkiyamāṉa kāriyaṅkaḷit tīrmāṉañceyya atikārikaḷ jaṉaṅkaḷi allatu jaṉattalivarkaḷi ōriṭattil varavaḻittu avaruṭaṉ rājīya viṣayaṅkaḷippaṟṟip pēcuvatē aṉusamyāṉam eṉṟu cila vitvāṉkaḷ kūṟuvatu poruntumeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. mūṉṟām cāsaṉattiṉpaṭi aṉusamyāṉattiṉ nōkkamāvatu, “atikārikaḷ taṅkaḷ vēliyic carivara muṭippatum tarmatti ellōrukkum pōtippatumē”. pāṭalipurattic cuṟṟi aṉusamyāṉamāṉatu aintu āṇṭukaḷukku oru muṟiyum, ujjayiṉi - takṣacilā mākāṇaṅkaḷil mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷukku orumuṟiyum, tosāli mākāṇattil aintu āṇṭukaḷukku oru muṟiyum naṭantu vantateṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. iccollukku nām koḷḷum karuttu cariyāyiṉ, acōkaṉ āḷuki muṟṟilum ēka cakkarātipattiyamā yirukkavilliyeṉṟum jaṉaṅkaḷuṭiya cammatattuṭaṉ pala kāriyaṅkaḷ naṭantaṉaveṉṟum ūkikkalām.

turittaṉattil mekāsteṉis kūṟiyiruppatu pōṉṟa kamiṭṭikaḷ amintiruntaṉaveṉṟum cāsaṉaṅkaḷ aṟivikkiṉṟaṉa. kaṇaṉā, pariṣat, nikāyā mutaliya nirvāka capikaḷ mūṉṟām cāsaṉattilum, paṉṉiraṇṭām cāsaṉattilum, vēṟiṭaṅkaḷilum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl avaṟṟiṉ amippum kaṭamikaḷum eṉṉaveṉṟu viḷaṅkavilli.

acōkaṉuṭiya perumi avaṉuṭiya rājīya nōkkaṅkaḷiṟ piratipalikkiṉṟatu, avvaracaṉuṭiya putiya rājīya nōkkaṅkaḷum, avaṉāl turittaṉattil ceyyappaṭṭa cīrtiruttaṅkaḷum avaṉukkup piṉ nili niṉṟatākat tōṉṟavilli. acōkaṉuṭiya cantatiyār avaṉuṭiya uṟcākattiyum uyarnōkkaṅkaḷiyum urimiyākak koḷḷavilli. āyiṉum acōkaṉiṉ aracāṭci ekkālamum ulakacarittirattil mukkiya ilakkiyamāyirukkum eṉpatu teḷivu.

acōkak kalveṭṭukaḷ akappaṭum iṭaṅkaḷi ﻿

⁠acōka

ēkātipattiyat

tiṉ virivu

﻿ātāramāka vittukkoṇṭu acōkaṉuṭiya ēkātipattiyattiṉ virivi nām ēṟakkuṟiyat tīrmāṉikkalām. mēlum, cantirakuptaṉuṭiyakālattil ēkātipattiyam aṭintirunta viriviliruntu acōkaṉ kālattil ēkātipattiyattiṉ virivu kuṟivupaṭavilli. parmāvit tavirttuḷḷa intiya rājyattiviṭa acōkaṉrājyam atika virivāyiruntatu. atil teṉ kōṭiyiluḷḷa tamiḻnāṭṭu mūvēntarum aṅkuḷḷa vēṟu aracarum uṭpaṭavilli ; āyiṉum taṟkālattil intiyāvukkup puṟamāyirukkum palūccistāṉam, apkāṉistāṉam ataṟku appāluḷḷa hintukkuṣ malittoṭarvariyumuḷḷa piratēcam ivi acōkaṉ sāmrājyattil akappaṭṭiruntaṉa. vaṭakkē kācmīramum nēppāḷamum kiḻakkē assām variyumuḷḷa nāṭukaḷum, teṟkē micūr variyumuḷḷa takkaṇamum anta sāmrājyattil aṭaṅkiyiruntaṉa. rāṣṭirikar, pitēṉikar, pulintar, āntrar mutaliya jaṉaṅkaḷ acōkaṉuṭiya ātipattiyattukku uṭpaṭṭavarkaḷāyiruntālum avarkaḷ atika cutantirattip peṟṟiruntaṉar. intiyāviṉ puṟamē mattiya āciyāviluḷḷa kōpippāli vaṉappiratēcattuḷḷa kōṭṭāṉ allatu cīṉa turkkistāṉam akkālattil vaḷamum nākarikamum taḻitta tēcamāyiruntatu. ippiratēcam acōkaṉ āḷukikku uṭpaṭṭirukkavilliyāyiṉum ekkāraṇattālō acōkaṉuṭiya celvākku aṅku atika valimiyuṭiya tāyiruntatu. ataṟku acōkaṉuṭiya tarmap piracāram allāmal vēṟu kāraṇaṅkaḷum irukkalām. viyāpārap pōkkuvaravu muṟkālaṅkaḷilēyē ēṟpaṭṭiruntatu. acōkaṉ kālamutal, kalvi kēḷvikaḷ viṣayamākavum atikamāṉa pōkkuvaravu ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. acōkaṉ makaṉāṉa kuṉālaṉ kōṭṭāṉ jaṉaṅkaḷāl aracaṉākat terinteṭukkappaṭṭāṉeṉṟum putta matam avaṉ mūlamāka appakkaṅkaḷil parantateṉṟum tivyāvatāṉattil kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ippiratēcam akkālattil ceḻittōṅkiya pala stūpaṅkaḷālum vihāraṅkaḷālum viḷaṅkiyatāka yuvaṉcuvaṅ kūṟukiṟāṉ.



↑ * epikrāpiyā intikkā (Epigraphia Indica) - Vol. viii. pakkam, 36.





kuṟippukaḷ





V. akkālattup paḻamporuḷkaḷ



namakku akappaṭṭuḷḷa paṇṭikkālac cāmāṉkaḷil acōkaṉ kālattukku muṟpaṭṭavi atikamilli. intiyāviṉ paḻiya carittira ārāyccikku iḵtu oru peruṅkuṟivē. ﻿

ivaṟṟilum

paḻamiyāṉavi

intiyāvil

kiṭiyā

⁠



ekiptu, assiriyā, kirīs, krīṭ mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷil namakku akappaṭṭuḷḷa paṇṭikkālac cāmāṉkaḷil acōkaṉ kālattukku muṟpaṭṭavi atikamilli. intiyāviṉ paḻiya carittira ārāyccikku iḵtu oruperuṅkuṟivē. ekiptu, assiriyā, kirīs, krīṭ mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷil ki. mu. 2000 allatu 2500 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa paḻamporuḷkaḷ akappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl maḻi mikutiyiṉālum intiyāviṉ cītōṣṇa niliyiṉ vicēṣattālum avvaḷavu purātaṉamāṉa poruḷkaḷ nam nāṭṭil atikamāka akappaṭavilli, acōkaṉ kālattukku muṉ vīṭukaḷum araṇmaṉikaḷum kōyilkaḷum ellāvita kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum marattiṉālēyē ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. iv viṣayam nam nāṭṭil mikappurātaṉamāṉa poruḷkaḷ akappaṭātataṟku vēṟu orukāraṇam, acōkaṉ kālattil allatu ataṟkuc caṟṟu muṉputāṉ intiya jaṉaṅkaḷ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷukkuk kaṟkaḷi upayōkikkat toṭaṅkiṉar. acōkaṉ kaṟkaḷi marakkaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷukku upakaraṇamāka upayōkappaṭuttiyirukkalām. acōkaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ eṇṇiṟantaṉaveṉṟa

﻿

acōkaṉuṭiya

vēlikaḷ

﻿



jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ nampikkiyik kuṟippiṭṭu, pāhiyaṉ eṉṟa cīṉayāttiri, mūṉṟē mūṉṟu varuṣaṅvaruṣaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇkaḷukkut teṉpaṭāta yakṣar kiṉṉarar pōṉṟa teyvaṅkaḷiṉ utaviyikkoṇṭu aracaṉ eṇpattu nālāyiram stāpaṉaṅkaḷik kaṭṭuvittatākak kūṟukiṟāṉ. acōkaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭavi stūpaṅkaḷ maṭṭumalla ; kācmīrattiṉ talinakaramākiya srīnakarum nēppāḷattiṉ talinakaramākiya lalitāpaṭṭaṇamum acōkaṉāl stāpikkappaṭṭateṉṟu muṉṉē coṉṉōm. nālantāvum ikkālattil uṟpattiyāyiṟṟu. pāṭṉāviṉ camīpam acōkaṉ araṇmaṉi iruntateṉṟu ﻿

ivaṟṟiṉ

taṟkāla

aṟikuṟikaḷ

﻿camīpakālattil kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭirukkiṟatu. 1913 –l pampāy nakarattuk kōṭisrīmāṉkaḷākiya ṭāṭṭā vamcattāriṉ paṇa utaviyiṉ kāraṇamāka aṅkē purātaṉa vastu ārāycci toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu, talistāṉattuḷḷa sapāmaṇṭapam āstāṉa maṇṭapam mutaliya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ pala tariyit tōṇṭip pārttatil akappaṭṭaṉa. pāracīka tēcattup paḻiya araṇmaṉikaḷiṉ ciṟpa vicēṣaṅkaḷ iṅkum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa ; ātalāl iṅkuḷḷa vēlippāṭu pārasīka ciṟpikaḷuṭiyatāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu aṉumāṉik kappaṭukiṟatu.

oru kālattil acōkaṉuṭiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ intiyāviṉ ellāppākaṅkaḷilum mikuntiruntaṉa. acōkaṉukkup piṟkālattil makatatēcattukku pihār eṉṟu peyar vanta kāraṇam annāṭu muḻuvatum putta vihāraṅkaḷāl niṟintiruntamiyē eṉalām. āṉāl taṟkālam acōkaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ ivvulakattiluḷḷa pala māyikaḷippōla aḻintu maṟintupōyirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ aṟikuṟiyāyk kiṭittuḷḷa purātaṉa vastukkaḷi iṅku, vivarippōm.

stūpam eṉṟāl putta matattic cērnta periyōrukkuk ﻿

stūpaṅkaḷ

﻿kalliṉāl kaṭṭappaṭum camāti. iti, puttar paḷḷi yeṉṟum kūṟalām. muṉivariṉ cāmpali stūpattiṉ mēṟpuṟattil aṭakkam ceyvārkaḷ, uruvattil stūpam oru perum kōḷattiṉ pātiyi uyarnta pīṭattiṉ mēl kaviḻttatupōlirukkum. stūpam karuṅkalliṉālō cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷālō kaṭṭappaṭṭatāyirukkalām. stūpattiṉ puṟamē viḷakku māṭaṅkaḷum vēṟu alaṅkāraṅkaḷum irukkum. mēṟpuṟattil iṅkilīṣ eḻuttu T pōṉṟa aṭiyāḷamum ataṟku mēl paranta oru kuṭiyum nāṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. toṇṭar stūpattip piratakṣiṇam ceytuvara pirākāramum, pirākārattiṉ nāṉku puṟamum peruṅkaṟkaḷiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa vēliyum vēliyiṉ puṟamē kōpuravācalkaḷippōla mikka alaṅkāramāyc ceyyappaṭṭa vācalkaḷum citivupaṭāta stūpaṅkaḷil kāṇalām ippaṭippaṭṭa stūpam oṉṟu nalla stitiyil sāñci eṉṟa ūril irukkiṟatu. ivvūr mattiya intiyāviccērnta pōpāl camstāṉattil uḷḷatu. aṅku iraṇṭu mūṉṟu stūpaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṟ piratāṉamāṉa stūpam acōkaṉ kālattatu eṉṟu aṉumāṉikkappaṭukiṟatu. vācalkaḷ piṉ kaṭṭappaṭṭaviyāyiṉum mutalāvatu kṣēttiram pūrāvum marattiṉāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkalām. piṟkālattu aracar tamakku iyaṉṟavāṟu itēvēlikaḷikkaṟkaḷil ceytirukkalām.



sāñciyiluḷḷa piratāṉa stūpam ātiyil cumār ﻿

sāñci stūpam

﻿eḻupatteṭṭaṭi uyarntiruntatu. taṟkālam ataṉmēluḷḷa alaṅkāraṅkaḷ pōyviṭṭaṉa ; ātalāl atu aimpattu nāṉku aṭi uyaramirukkiṟatu. ikkaṭṭiṭattiṉ mika aḻakāṉa vēlippāṭu nāṉku ticikaḷiyum nōkki niṟkum vācalkaḷām. ivi muppattu nāṉku aṭi uyarntu mikak kampīramāṉa tōṟṟattuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivvācalkaḷiṉ mēlpākattiṟ kalliṉāṟ palatōraṇaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ilēcāṉa marakkaṭṭiyik kiyāḷuvatil taccaṉukku evvaḷavu kilākavamum tiṟamum nuṭpamum ēṟpaṭalāmō appaṭippaṭṭa lākavamum nuṭpamum nām aṅkuḷḷa kalciṟpaṅkaḷil kāṇkiṟōm. cila vācalkaḷ piṟkālattaviyāyiruntālum anta stūpatti acōkaṉ kālattuc ciṟpattiṟamikku mātiriyāka nām vittuk koḷḷalām.[1]

parhut eṉṟa ūriluḷḷa stūpamum acōkaṉ ﻿

parhut

stūpam

﻿kālattatē. itu sāñcikkuc cumār irunūṟu mil kiḻakkē uḷḷatu. stūpam cuṭṭaceṅkaṟkaḷiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatu ; āyiṉum puṟamuḷḷa kalvēliyiṉ kaṟkaḷil cetukkappaṭṭa ciṟpaṅkaḷ mika arumiyāṉavi. vēliyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷellām akkālattu naṭi uṭi pāvaṉikaḷi viḷakkum ciṟpaṅkaḷāl niṟintirukkiṉṟaṉa. jātakak katikaḷ eṉpavi keḷatama puttarukku avaruṭiya muṉ jaṉmaṅkaḷil ēṟpaṭṭa aṉupavaṅkaḷik kūṟum katikaḷeṉṟu puttarkaḷāl karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avikaḷiṟ pala kuraṅku, yāṉi, mutali, māṉ, kākam, kōḻi mutaliya pirāṇikaḷip paṟṟiya īsāppiṉ katikaḷ pōṉṟavi. parhut stūpattiṉ vēlikkaṟkaḷiluḷḷa ciṟpaṅkaḷ pala jātakak katikaḷiyum puttariṉ vāḻnāṭkaḷil naṭanta campavaṅkaḷiyum viḷakkac cetukkappaṭṭa cittiraṅkaḷām. ac ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ kīḻ, “mikajātakam”, “nāka(yāṉi)jātakam,” “ṣaṭtanti jātakam” “hamsa jātakam,” “kiṉṉara jātakama,” “kukkuṭa jātakam,” “makātēva jātakam,” “jaṉakarāja jātakam” eṉṟu ciṟpattiṉ viṣayam kallil veṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. ik kalveṭṭukaḷ intiyāviṉ vēṟu pala pākaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa cittiraṅkaḷiṉ karutti viḷakka mikavum upayōkappaṭṭaṉa.

kāciyiṉ arukil uḷḷa sārnātattil 1905 ﻿

sārnāt

stūpam

﻿varuṣattil purātaṉa vastu ārāycci toṭaṅkiyapōtu aṅkup putintu kiṭanta stūpam teriyavantatu. anta stūpam sāñci stūpatti viṭap periyatu; ceṅkalliṉāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭatu.

putta kayiyil acōkaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa stūpam ﻿

kayi stūpam

﻿pala nāḷ variyil iruntatu. anta stūpattiṉ puṟavēliyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷ maṭṭum ippōtu aṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. acōkaṉuṭiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ avaciṣṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ ataṟkup piṟkālattu vēlippāṭṭiṉ pākaṅkaḷum kalantu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.

acōkaṉ kālattiṟ puttarukku vikrakaṅkaḷ kiṭiyā.āṉāl puttaruṭiyavum avaruṭiya tarmat ﻿

stūpaṅkaḷil

uṇṭāṉa

māṟutalkaḷ

﻿tiṉuṭiyavum, aṟikuṟiyākac cila aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. toṇṭar inta aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷi vaṇaṅki vantaṉar. avaṟṟil mukkiyamāṉatu pōti virukṣam. mirukaṅkaḷālum maṉitarkaḷālum teyvaṅkaḷālum virukṣaṅkaḷ vaṇaṅkappaṭuvati sāñci, parhut iṅkuḷḷa ciṟpaṅkaḷil nām mikutiyāyp pārkkalām. cakkaraṅkaḷum vekuvāka kaṟkaḷil cetukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi puttar tarmatti ulakattiṟkup pōtikkat toṭaṅkiyatiṉ aṟikuṟi. iraṇṭu kālaṭiyiṇikaḷ cittirikkappaṭṭu avaṟṟic cuṟṟi niṟkum palar ivaṟṟip pōṟṟi vaṇaṅkum ciṟpattiyum cila iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇalām. ituvum puttariṉ ciṉṉamē. mahāyāṉa puttamatam intiyāviṟ pirapalamāṉatum stūpaṅkaḷ puttariṉ kōyilkaḷāy māṟiṉa. puttarukkup palavitap piratimikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. stūpattiṉ puṟakkalliṉ naṭuvil puttaruṭiya uruvam cetukkappaṭṭu namatu intuk kōyilkaḷil naṭappatupōla ataṉ muṉ pūji apiṣēkam tūpatīpaṅkaḷ mutaliya caṭaṅkukaḷum naṭakkat toṭaṅkiṉa. stūpattiyē maṟṟoru periya aṟiyiṉ ōrattil kaṭṭi muḻuvatum oru puttak kōyilāy māṟṟappaṭṭatu. ippaṭippaṭṭa kōyilkaḷē cittiyam allatu paḷḷi eṉappaṭum.

acōkaṉāl kuṭiyappaṭṭa kukikaḷippaṟṟi ﻿

kukikaḷ

﻿atikamāyc collavēṇṭiyatilli. mika uṟutiyāṉa karuṅkallil pala tuṟavikaḷ cērntu vācam ceyyumpaṭi vicālamāṉa aṟikaḷ maliyōraṅkaḷil kuṭiyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. uṭpuṟam mika naṉṟāy iḻikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.

acōkaṉāl nāṭṭappaṭṭa stampaṅkaḷ, avaṉuṭiya aracāṭcikku mika uyarnta ñāpakac ciṉṉaṅkaḷeṉṟu collalām, acōkaṉ kālattukku muṉṉamē ippaṭippaṭṭa stampaṅkaḷ

stampaṅkaḷ

iruntaṉaveṉṟu cāsaṉaṅkaḷiliruntu teriyavarukiṟatu.[2] acōkaṉ kālattukkup piṉṉum stāpikkappaṭṭa stampaṅkaḷ pala uḷḷaṉa. nūṟu varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ kūṭa ippōtiruppatilum pala atikamāyiruntaṉa. ittakiya tūṇkaḷiṉ perumiyic cātāraṇa jaṉaṅkaḷ uṇarāmal avi tāṟumāṟāy upayōkikkappaṭṭaṉa. stampaṅkaḷiṉ tuṇṭukaḷ cālikaḷil parappum caraḷi aṭittuc camappaṭutta ṟōlarkaḷākavum karumpāliyil uralkaḷākavum upayōkappaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.

ippōtu kāṇappaṭum acōka stampaṅkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭu. avaṟṟil pattu stampaṅkaḷil acōkaṉuṭiya likitam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. iraṇṭil oru likitamumilliyāyiṉum stampaṅkaḷ acōkaṉ kālattaviyē. hintustāṉap piratēcattukku veḷiyil eṅkum acōkaṉuṭiya stampaṅkaḷ kiṭittila.





* * *



acōka stampaṅkaḷiṉ vivaraṇam⁠  aṅku kāṇappaṭum likitaṅkaḷ,



* * *

2. ṭilli - mīrat stampam.⁠  ⁠௸ ⁠1-6

3. pirayāki stampam.  sārnāt cāsaṉattiṉ

⁠  pakuti.rāṇikāruvākiyiṉ

⁠  likitam, stampacāsaṉaṅ

⁠  kaḷ⁠1-9

4. sārcāt stampam.⁠  sārnāt cāsaṉam.

ellā stampaṅkaḷum vēlippāṭṭil oṉṟu pōliruttalāl avaṟṟip potuvāy vivarikkalām. stampattiṟku aṭiyil caturamāṉa pīṭamum, mēlē vicitramāyk kaṇṭāmaṇi pōṉṟa cikaramum, cikarattiṉmēl vaṭṭamākavō caturamākavō uḷḷa ōraṭi kaṉamuṭiya taṭṭum, attaṭṭiṉmēl oṉṟu allatu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, allatu nāṉku ciṅkaṅkaḷuṭiya piratimi stampattiṉ makuṭamākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. cila stampaṅkaḷil makuṭam viruṣapam allatu karuṭaṉ piratimiyā yirukkiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟiṉ uyaram 40 al latu50 aṭi.

atikamāyk keṭāmalum cuvastāṉattilum irukkum acōka ﻿

ivaṟṟiṉ

ciṟpattiṟami

﻿stampaṅkaḷ iraṇṭu mūṉṟē, maṟupakkattil kāṇappaṭum luriyā nantaṉkar stampam mika aḻakāṉatu. ataṉ cikaram auraṅkasīp kālattiṟkuṇṭu paṭṭu koñcam citivupaṭ ṭiruk





luriyā nantaṉkar stampam



citivuppaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu ; āyiṉum stampattiṉ aḻaku keṭavilli. pakhirā stampam itiviṭap peritāyiṉum vēlip pāṭṭil ituvē ciṟantatu. itaṉ uyaram 40 aṭi. cikaram maṭṭum cumār 7 aṭi. mattiya pākam urukkō veṇkalamō eṉṟu mayaṅkumpaṭi iḻikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu, cikarattiṉ mēluḷḷa taṭṭu vaṭṭavaṭivam. ataṉ viḷimpil aṉṉaṅkaḷ tam iriyik kottit tiṉṉuvatik kāṭṭum cittiram veku aḻakāyc cetukkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. inta stampam caṉṉār maliyiluḷḷa karuṅkalliṉāl ākkappaṭṭatu aṅkiruntu eppaṭi iti veṭṭi vēṟākkiṉārkaḷ ? entak kal taccar itic cetukki iḻittaṉar? entac ciṟpikaḷ itaṉ cikarattic cittirittaṉar ? ik kēḷvikaḷukkuc cariyāṉa viṭi nām iṅkuc colla muṭiyātu. āṉāl ippaṇiceytōr yāvarukkum kimuṭuku uriyateṉṟu nām collalām. samīpattiṟ kiṭittuḷḷa sārnāt stampattiṉ cikaram mikavum cirēṣṭamāṉa vēlippāṭu amintatu. ataṉ paṭatti 70-m pakkattil kāṇalām. stam ttiṉ makuṭam nāṉku ciṅkaṅkaḷ cērntu uṭkārntu koṇṭu tarma cakkarattit tamatu mutukil cumantu koṇṭiruppatupōl uḷḷatu. ciṅkaṅkaḷ jīvaṉuḷḷaṉa pōlavum mikak kampīramākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa.

acōkaṉ kālattuc ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ kuṇākuṇaṅkaḷi ﻿

intac ciṟpat

tip paṟṟiya

⁠matippu

﻿vitvāṉkaḷ palvitamāka nitāṉikkiṟārkaḷ. persiyā tēcattu aracarkaḷum tam nāṭṭil stampaṅkaḷi nāṭṭiṉar. avaṟṟi acōkaṉuṭiya stampaṅkaḷukkup palavitattilum oppiṭalām.



acōkaṉ araṇmaṉiyum maṟṟak kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum intiya ciṟpikaḷiṉ vēliyallaveṉṟum kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ vēlippāṭṭil intiyāvukku anniyamāṉa tēcaṅkaḷukkuriya alaṅkāraṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭuvatu itaṟkuc cāṉṟu eṉṟum apippirāyaṅkaḷ purātaṉa vastucāstirikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. nām iti oppukkoḷḷa muṭiyavilli. ēṉeṉṟāl, stampaṅkaḷilum kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷilumuḷḷa alaṅkāraṅkaḷ intiyāvukku anniyamāṉavi yeṉṟu sarva sammatamāy nirūpikkappaṭavilli. ellāt tēcaṅkaḷukkum iyaṟkiyāṉa saṅkītamum ilakkiyamum iruppatupōlac ciṟpamum iyaṟkiyākavē ēṟpaṭukiṟatu. aṭuttatu kāṭṭum paḷiṅkupōla ciṟpaṅkaḷum antanta nāṭṭāriṉ vāḻviyum maṉōpāvaṅkaḷiyum piratipalikkacceykiṉṟaṉa. intiyāvukkumaṭṭum ippaṭippaṭṭa cuyamāṉa ciṟpam illiyeṉṟu colla muṭiyumā?

intiyā kavarṉmeṇṭiṉ purātaṉavastu paricōtakaril mukkiyarākiya ṭākṭar mārṣal turiyum acōkaṉ kālattuc ciṟpam anniya maṉitariṉ vēliyeṉṟu matikkiṉṟār ; āṉālum ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ vēlippāṭṭi atikamāka mecci eḻutukiṟār. avar sārnāt stampattip paṟṟi eḻutiyiruppatu piṉ varumāṟu : “ pūrva kālattu vēlikaḷuḷ ivvaḷavu uyarnta maṉōtarmamum ciṟpat tiṟamiyum kāṇappaṭum vēli vēṟu ettēcattum kiṭiyātu eṉṟu nāṉ collat tuṇikiṉṟēṉ.” inta matippu sārnāt stampattiṉ cikarattukkumaṭṭumaṉṟu, potuvāka ellā acōka stampaṅkaḷukkum amiyumeṉpatu namatu tuṇipu. acōkaṉ kālattukkumuṟpaṭṭa ciṟpam atikamāka akappaṭṭila; āyiṉum nām mutalāvatāka intiyāviṉ ciṟpatti nōkkum pōtu atu kuḻantip paruvattatāyirāmal ārampattilēyē pūrṇa yuvaṉattuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itiliruntu nam nāṭṭil ciṟpattiṉ apivirtti veku kālattukku muṉṉamē toṭaṅkiviṭṭateṉṟum acōkaṉ kālattukkumuṉ maravēliyil aṭinta tiṟami, kaṭiciyākak kalvēlikku upayōkappaṭuttappaṭṭateṉṟum, ippaṭippaṭṭa cātaṉattiṉ vēṟupāṭṭiṉāl ciṟpattiṉ mēṉmi atikak kuṟivupaṭavilli yeṉṟum intiya ciṟpa apimāṉikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.



↑ 🞸 srī. ris ṭēviṭsa (Rhys Davids) eṉpavarāl eḻutappaṭṭa “putta kālattu intiyā” (Buddhist India) eṉṟa pustakattil ikkālattuc ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ paṭaṅkaḷ pala irukkiṉṟaṉa.

↑ mutal upacāsaṉam rūpanāt pāṭam, 7-m stampacāsaṉam.





kuṟippukaḷ





VI. acōka eḻuttu



acōkaṉ kālattil upayōkikkappaṭṭa lipi ﻿

iruvita purātaṉa

⁠lipikaḷ

﻿iruvakippaṭum. (i) perumpālāṉa intiya pāṣikaḷippōl iṭatu pakkattiliruntu valatu pakkattukku eḻutappaṭum prāmmi lipi. (ii) maṟṟoṉṟu hintustāṉiyippōla valatu pakkattiliruntu iṭatu pakkattukku eḻutappaṭum karōṣṭi lipi. prāmmi lipiyi intiyāviluḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷukkellām tāy eṉṟu collalām. perumpālāṉa acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷ inta lipiyilēyē eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum ivviruvaki lipikaḷi viṭap purātaṉamāṉa lipikaḷ intiyāvil illiyeṉṟu collalām.

intiyāviluḷḷa pāṣikaḷukku vevvēṟu lipi taṟkālattil ēṟpaṭṭiruntālum inta lipikaḷ acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷiluḷḷa prāmmi lipiyiliruntu





sārnāt stampattiṉ cikaram



uṟpattiyāṉaviyē. ivvuṇmi inta lipikaḷ ﻿

prāmmi lipi

intiya eḻuttuk

kaḷukku mūlā

⁠tāram﻿ellāvaṟṟiyum tāratammiyappaṭuttip paṭikkum pakṣattil eḷitil viḷaṅkum, mulā eḻuttiṉ vaṭivam eḻutuvataṟku upayōkikkappaṭum sātaṉaṅkaḷukkut takkapaṭiyum karuvikaḷukkut takkapaṭiyum lēkakaṉ carīra stitikkut takkapaṭiyum māṟutal aṭikiṉṟaṉa. ik kāraṇaṅkaḷāl eḻuttiṉ vaṭivam kālatēcaṅkaḷi aṉusarittup pala māṟutalkaḷ aṭivati nām cāsaṉa ārāycci mūlamāy aṟintukoḷḷalām. acōkaṉ kālattukku nūṟu varuṣaṅkaḷukkuppiṉ prāmmi lipiyil ceṅkuttāṉa kōṭukaḷ māṟi vaṭṭaṅkaḷum vaḷivukaḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. utāraṇamāka paṭṭiprōlu likitattil [1] ippaṭippaṭṭa vaḷivukaḷi nām atikamāyk kāṇkiṟōm, tamiḻ, teluṅku, kirantam, maliyāḷam, kaṉṉaṭam eṉṟa teṉmoḻikaḷiṉ eḻuttukkaḷ ௸ likitattil kāṇappaṭum lipiyiliruntu uṟpattiyāṉavi eṉpati teṉ intiyāvil vacikkum nām kavaṉikkalām.

intiyāviṉ pāṣikaḷ anēka āyira varuṣaṅkaḷāka ﻿

intiya pāṣika

kaḷukku lipi ēṟ

paṭṭa vitam

﻿oruvita lipiyum ēṟpaṭāmal virttiyaṭintu vantaṉa. ellāvita kirantaṅkaḷiyum canti colli maṉappāṭam ceyyum vaḻakkattāl intiya jaṉaṅkaḷukkut taṅkaḷ pāṣiyi eḻutuvataṟku lipi avaciyamillāmaliruntatu. appaṭiyāyiṉ intiyapāṣi kaḷukku eppōtu lipi ēṟpaṭṭatu? intak kēḷvikku srīpular eṉṟa purātaṉa vastu cāstiriyiṉ viṭi piṉvarumāṟu: cumār ki.mu. eṭṭām nūṟṟaṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil intiyāvukkum, ataṉ mēṟkiluḷḷa yūtarip pōṉṟa jātiyārukkum viyāpāra viṣapamāyk kaṭal yāttiriyum cāmāṉkaḷiṉ pōkkuvaravum muṉṉilum atikamāy ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. iccātiyāriṭamiruntē intiya jaṉaṅkaḷ eḻutum vittiyik kaṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar. ēṉeṉṟāl, ivar eḻutivanta lipiyilum prāmmi lipiyilum cila eḻuttukkaḷ vaṭivilum uccarippilum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa.

āṉāl prāmmiyi luḷḷa ellā eḻuttukkaḷum kaṭaṉākak kiṭittavi yallaveṉṟu srīpūlarum oppuk koḷḷukiṟār. intiyar taṅkaḷiṭamirunta putu uccarippukku avaciyamāṉa kuṟikaḷit tāmē ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭaṉar. viyāpārikaḷ tām kaṟṟa putu vittiyi mutalil ñāpakak kuṟippukaḷukku upayōkappaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. paṉiyōliyum pūrjapattiramum cempu pōṉṟa lōkattakaṭukaḷum innāṭṭil eḻutuvataṟku sātaṉaṅkaḷāyiṉa. lipi vaḻakkattil vanta piṟakum matak kirantaṅkaḷi ippaṭippaṭṭa sātaṉaṅkaḷil eḻutivippataṟku vētiyar tuṇiyavilli. kālakkiramattil matacampantamāṉa kirantaṅkaḷum eḻutappaṭṭu maṉitaṉ ñāpakacaktikku ēṟpaṭṭirunta aṉāvaciyamāṉa pāratti koñcaṅkoñcamāy jaṉaṅkaḷ kuṟittukkoṇṭaṉar.

karōṣṭi lipi yūtajātiyāriṭamiruntu kiṭittataṉṟu. ﻿

karōṣṭi lipi

﻿ki. mu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil intiyāviṉ vaṭamēṟkiluḷḷa piratēcaṅkaḷ persiyā tēcattāriṉ ātīṉattiliruntaṉa. intap piratēcaṅkaḷil ārmēyik eṉṟa persiyātēcattu lipi paraviṟṟu, karōṣṭi inta lipiyiliruntu uṟpattiyāṉatu. karōṣṭi lipi acōkaṉ kālattilum ataṟkuppiṉ cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ variyum pañcāpilum vaṭamēṟku mākāṇattilum, sintutēcattilum paraviyiruntapōtilum kaṭiciyil intiyāvilum ataṉ puṟamum muṟṟilum maṟintu pōyiṟṟu. mattiya āciyāviluḷḷa kōṭṭāṉ oru kālattil vaḷamiyum nākarikamum taḻitta tēcamāyiruntateṉṟu muṉṉē kūṟiṉōm. karōṣṭi, lipiyil eḻutappaṭṭa puttamatak kirantaṅkaḷum maṟṟak kirantaṅkaḷum ittēcattiliruntu camīpa kālattil putiyalāy akappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi ulakattukku mikavum arumiyāṉa celvameṉṟu vitvāṉkaḷāl matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.



↑ 🞸 paṭṭiprōlu kiruṣṇā natiyiṉ mukattil kuṇṭūr jillāviluḷḷa ūr. paṭṭiprōlu likitam Epigraphia Indica, Vol. II. pakkam 323-l kaṇṭukoḷḷavum, itaṉ kālam ki. pi. 200.





kuṟippukaḷ





VII. piriyatarci aracaṉ kalveṭṭukkaḷ



acōkaṉ nāḷil naṭanta campavaṅkaḷit tīrmāṉikkavum ﻿

ivaṟṟiṉ

pākupāṭu

﻿aracaṉ kālatti nirṇayikkavum acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷ evvaḷavu mukkiya ātāraṅkaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟu nām nirūpittāyviṭṭatu. iṉi ic cāsaṉaṅkaḷiyē nōkkavēṇṭum. namakku ituvariyum kiṭittuḷḷa acōka likitaṅkaḷ muppattu mūṉṟu. ivaṟṟil paṉṉiraṇṭu likitaṅkaḷil aracaṉ paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣam kuṟippiṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. iḵtaṉṟi vēṟu cila likitaṅkaḷilum kāla nirṇayam ceyvataṟku ātāraṅkaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. intac cāsaṉaṅkaḷ yāvum rājīya atikārikaḷuṭiyavō mantirikaḷuṭiyavō vākkiyaṅkaḷāyirāmal aracaṉ nāviliruntu vanta vākkiyaṅkaḷ eṉpatil aiyamilli; ēṉeṉṟāl aracaṉaṉṟi vēṟevarum, evvaḷavu cutantiramuṭiyavarāyiṉum, eḻutat tuṇiyāta vākkiyaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟi nām cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇalām. pala likitaṅkaḷum “tēvarkaḷukkup piriyaṉāṉa piyatavasi rājaṉ ippaṭic collukiṟāṉ” tēvāṉām piyō piya tasi lājā ēvam āhā eṉṟō, allatu ivvākkiyattik koñcam māṟṟiyō toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

acōkaṉuṭiya cāsaṉaṅkaḷ mikap putumiyāṉa ﻿

ivaṟṟiṉ

taṉmi

﻿tastāvējukaḷām. intiyāviluḷḷa vēṟu oru aracaṉum ippaṭippaṭṭa cāsaṉaṅkaḷi eḻutivittilaṉ. ic cāsaṉaṅkaḷum palavakippaṭṭaṉa. cila, aracaṉ taṉ atikārikaḷukku aṉuppiya kaṭṭaḷikaḷiṉ piratikaḷ pōlirukkiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟavi aracaṉ jaṉaṅkaḷukku aṉuppum viḷamparaṅkaḷeṉṟu tōṉṟum. tarmap piracārattil aracaṉ koṇṭa mikutiyāṉa uṟcākattiṉāl palacāsaṉaṅkaḷil aracaṉ jaṉaṅkaḷukkut tarmattip pōtikkiṟāṉ. ivaṟṟi aracaṉ caṭṭaṅkaḷ eṉṟu koḷḷakkūṭātu. ippōtaṉikaḷip pirajikaḷ mīṟi naṭakkiṟatippaṟṟi aracaṉ viyacaṉamaṭintālum jaṉaṅkaḷik kaṭṭāyappaṭuttavō mīṟutalukkuc cikṣikaḷ vitikkavō virumpiṉatāka nām eṇṇa iṭamilli.

inta liki taṅkaḷuṭiya vācakarīti ciṟcila iṭaṅkaḷiṟ curuk ﻿

vācakarīti

﻿ curukkamāka amintirukkiṟateṉṟum maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil virivāyirukkiṟateṉṟum acōkaṉ kūṟukiṟāṉ (patiṉāṉkām cāsaṉam). curukkamāka amintirukkiṟa pākaṅkaḷ illiyeṉṟē collalām. mika virintu taḷarnta vācakarītiyē ivaṟṟiṉ vicēṣakuṇam. curuṅkac colli viḷaṅka vittalukkum inta likitaṅkaḷukkum veku tūramuṇṭu. āyiṉum acōkaṉ, viṣayattiṉuṭiya tēṉ pōṉṟa māturiyattālēyē ivvitam vivarikkavum tiruppic collavum tūṇṭappaṭṭatākac colvatu nāmum oppuk koḷḷak kūṭiyatē. samarasa pāvattippaṟṟiya paṉṉiraṇṭām cāsaṉam, tarmattiṉ veṟṟiyip paṟṟiya patiṉmūṉṟām cāsaṉam, aracaṉ naṟceykikaḷ palavaṟṟi eṭuttuk kūṟum ēḻām stampa cāsaṉam, ivaṟṟi nām vācikkum pōtu aracaṉuṭiya uyar nōkkaṅkaḷiyē nām kuṟippiṭukiṟōm. ivikaḷiṉ ēṟṟamāṉatu vācakarītiyiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷi maṟakkac ceykiṉṟatu.

acōkaṉāl eḻutappaṭṭavi yellām cāsaṉaṅkaḷ alla. ﻿

cāsaṉaṅkaḷ

allāta likitaṅ

⁠kaḷ

﻿utāraṇamāka, rummiṉ tēyīyiluḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷum kukikaḷiluḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷum cāsaṉaṅkaḷ. eṉṟa peyarukku uriyaviyalla; avaṟṟi likitaṅkaḷ eṉṟu maṭṭumē collamuṭiyum.

acōka likitaṅkaḷ ellā vakiyum ellāp piratikaḷum ﻿

likitaṅkaḷiṉ

⁠pirivu

﻿cērntu 140 eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭalām. ovvoru likitattukkumuḷḷa pala piratikaḷi vevvēṟāka eṇṇiṉāl ikkaṇakku ēṟpaṭukiṟatu. pirattiyēkamāṉa tastāvējukaḷ muppattu mūṉṟu; avaṟṟi oṉpatu pirivukaḷākap pirikkalām:



I. upa cāsaṉaṅkaḷ. ⁠2

ivi aracaṉ puttamatattil cērntataṉ

piṉ mutalil piracuram ceyyappaṭṭavi.

akappaṭṭuḷḷa acōkak kalveṭṭuk

kaḷuḷ iviyē muntiyavi pōlum.

II. pāpru cāsaṉam. ⁠1

putta saṅkattiluḷḷa talivarukku acōkaṉ

eḻutiya kaṭitam. pāpruvil akappaṭṭiruppatāl

pāpru cāsaṉam eṉṟu itaṟku srī viṉseṉṭ

smit peyar vaḻaṅkukiṉṟār.

III. patiṉāṉku mukkiya cāsaṉaṅkaḷ. ⁠14

ivi acōkaṉ tarmattiyum avaṉuṭiya

nōkkaṅkaḷiyum vivarikkiṉṟaṉa.

IV. kaliṅka cāsaṉaṅkaḷ. ⁠2

putitākap piṭikkappaṭṭa kaliṅka tēcattu

jaṉaṅkaḷukkum ataṉ pakkattiluḷḷa

kāṭṭu jātiyārukkum apaya tāṉam

ceyyum cāsaṉaṅkaḷ.

V. stampa cāsaṉaṅkaḷ. ⁠7

aracaṉatu aracāṭciyiṉ taṉmiyip paṟṟi

yum koḷkikaḷip paṟṟiyum kaṭici

muṟiyāka piracuram ceyyappaṭṭavi.

VI. sārkāt cāsaṉam. ⁠1

putta saṅkatti uṭpakikaḷiliruntu

kāppāṟṟuvataṟku iṭappaṭṭa kaṭṭaḷi.

sāñci stampattilum pirayāki stam

pattilum iccāsaṉattiṉ cila vākkiyaṅ

kaḷ eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.

rummiṉtēyīyilum niklīvāvilum acōka

ṉatu tīrttayāttiriyiṉ ñāpakakkuṟippāka

eḻutappaṭṭavi.

VIII.. rāṇi kāruvākiyiṉ likitam.  1

iṅku acōkaṉ rāṇi kāruvāki, taṉatu

tāṉaṅkaḷi vicēṣamāyk kavaṉikka

vēṇṭu meṉkiṟāḷ.

IX. tāṉappiramāṇa likitaṅkaḷ.  3

ivi ājīvakar vācam ceyvataṟku eṉṟu

kuṭiyappaṭṭa kukikaḷil uḷḷavi.
