II



puttamaṟi nūlkaḷ



acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷilum ip pustakattiṉ avatārikiyilum kūṟappeṟṟa putta nūlkaḷip paṟṟic curuṅka viḷakkuvatu ivvaṉupantattiṉ nōkkamākum. putta camayattic cērnta nūlkaḷ eṇṇiṟantaṉa eṉṟu kūṟuvatu mikiyalla. puttamatamāṉatu kāla aḷavil ceḻittōṅkip pala kappum kavaṟukaḷumuṭiya arumperum virukṣamāka vaḷara, ataṉ camaya nūlkaḷum avvitamē palavāṟākavum paṟpala pāṣikaḷilum tōṉṟiṉa. samskiruta moḻiyil pala putta nūlkaḷ uṇṭu. tipet, cīṉā, ilaṅki mutaliya tēca pāṣikaḷilum pala uḷa. avaṟṟil cila nūlkaḷ pakavatkīti, kaṭōpaṉiṣattu mutaliya nūlkaḷippōla ulakattiṉ matippukkum cācuvatamāṉa pukaḻukkum uriyavi, āṉāl nām iṅkuk kūṟappōvatu annūlkaḷippaṟṟi alla ; putta camayattiṉ mūla nūlkaḷ eṉṟu kaṇikkak kūṭiyavi tripiṭaka eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟa kirantaṅkaḷām. iṅku avaṟṟil aṭaṅkiṉavum ‘puttarkaḷ tirumaṟi’ eṉṟu collattakuntaṉavumāṉa nūlkaḷi vivarippōm.

kutama puttarum avaruṭiya cīṣarkaḷum taṅkaḷuṭiya mataviṣayamāṉa campāṣaṇikaḷukku avvappōtu vaṭa intiyāvil vaḻaṅkivanta pirākiruta moḻikaḷiyē upayōkittaṉar. ataṉāl āti putta nūlkaḷiṉ pāṣi pirākirutamoḻiyāyiruntatu. ippāṣi taṟkālam pāli eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ilaṅki, parmā mutaliya teṉ nāṭukaḷil pāli puṇṇiyapāṣi eṉak karutappaṭṭu annāṭṭup paṇṭitarkaḷāl pōṟṟappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. mūṉṟu piṭakaṅkaḷil aṭaṅkiya kirantaṅkaḷukku uriyākavum viyākkiyāṉaṅkaḷākavum vēṟu pala nūlkaḷ pāliyil eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.

piṭakam eṉṟa col kūṭi eṉṉum poruḷuṭiyatu, tripiṭaka eṉṟāl mūṉṟu mukkiyamāṉa puttap pirapantat tiraṭṭukaḷām. itu pāliyil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iḵtu accil innūliṉ pakkaṅkaḷippōla patiṉāyiram pakkaṅkaḷukku mēṟpaṭum; ‘pipiḷ' eṉṟa kiṟistuvarkaḷuṭiya cattiyavētap puttakatti viṭa mummaṭaṅku virivāṉatu. itil aṭaṅkiya kiran taṅkaḷ irupattoṉpatu eṉṟu collalām. inta irupattoṉpatil pala kirantaṅkaḷ ciṟu urikaḷiṉ tokutiyēyām.

mūṉṟu piṭakaṅkaḷil aṭaṅkiya irupattoṉpatu nūlkaḷiyum putta camayattiṉ mūla nūlkaḷ eṉalām. avaṟṟiṟ kūṟappaṭum camayakkoḷkikaḷē tēravātam (samskirutam: stavira vātam) allatu periyōriṉ matam eṉṟu karutappaṭupavi. kutama puttar nirvāṇatti aṭinta ciṟitu kālattiṟkuḷḷēyē putta saṅkattiṉ koḷkiyil vēṟupāṭukaḷ pala uṇṭāyiṉa. cilar tēravātak kakṣikku etirāka niṉṟaṉar. acōkacakkaravarttiyiṉ kālamvariyum, puttarkaḷukkiṭiyil entac camaya nūlkaḷ tamatu maṟikkup puṟam eṉṟum, evi uṭpaṭṭavi eṉṟum tīrmāṉam peṟā mal vātaṅkaḷ naṭantu vantaṉa. mūṉṟāvatu putta makāsapiyil ivviṣayam oruvāṟu muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. camaya nūlkaḷ tiraṭṭappaṭṭu varicippaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ivvivaraṅkaḷi - avatārikiyil 37-m pakkattil kūṟiyuḷḷōm.

aṉēka puttarkaḷ am makāsapiyil tērteṭukkappaṭṭa ellāc camaya nūlkaḷiyum oppuk koḷḷavilliyāyiṉum potuvāka avi cākkiya muṉivaruṭiya tarmōpatēcaṅkaḷi viḷakkukiṉṟaviyē eṉpatu maṭṭil oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭatu, ivvapippirāyatti uṟutippaṭutta purātaṉa vastu ārāycci attāṭciyit tarukiṉṟatu. ki. mu. mūṉṟu allatu iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭic cērntaṉavum parhut eṉṟa viṭattiliruntu akappaṭṭuḷḷaṉavumāṉa cila kalveṭṭu kaḷil 'pañcanikāyikaṉ' (aintu nikāyaṅkaḷiyum kaṟṟut tērntavaṉ), pēṭaki (piṭakaṅkaḷik kaṟṟa vittuvāṉ) eṉṟa paṭṭaṅkaḷ putta pikṣukkaḷukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. piṭakam eṉṟa patam camaya nūlkaḷit tokuppataṟkumuṉ ēṟpaṭṭirukka muṭiyātu, puttaruṭiya vākkiyaṅkaḷi aintu nikāyaṅkaḷāka vakuttatum ātiyil ēṟpaṭṭataṉṟu. mūṉṟāvatu makāsapiyil maṟinūlkaḷ niccayikkappaṭṭa piṉpē iccoṟkaḷ vaḻakkil vantirukka iṭamuṇṭu, ātalāl, immakāsapiyil nirṇayikkappaṭṭa nūlkaḷ akkālam potuvāka oppuk koḷḷappaṭṭavi eṉpatu teḷivākum. teṉ puttamatam, vaṭa puttamatam eṉṟa vittiyācaṅkaḷ appōtu pirapalamākavilli.

mūṉṟu piṭakaṅkaḷil aṭaṅkiya puttap pirapantaṅkaḷi vētattiṉ ñāṉakāṇṭattiṟku oppiṭalāmāyiṉum avi iraṇṭiṟkum nirampa vēṟupāṭukaḷum uḷa. āraṇyakaṅkaḷ upaniṣattukaḷ mutaliya maṟinūlkaḷ vevvēṟu kālattavi; paṟpala riṣikaḷiṉ mataṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiyavi; palavitak koḷkikaḷip piratipātippavi. piṭakaṅkaḷil aṭaṅkiya ' nūlkaḷō, kutama puttar koḷkikaḷi maṭṭil viḷakkuvaṉavākum.

ivaṟṟi, vicēṣamāka iraṇṭāvatu piṭakattilaṭaṅkiya nūlkaḷi, kiṟistavarkaḷiṉ cattiyavētattil aṭaṅkiya pustakaṅkaḷukku oppiṭuvatu caṟṟu poruntum. pipiḷiṉ ‘ putu ēṟpāṭu' eṉṟa pakutiyil kiṟistu muṉivariṉ varalāṟum avar upatēcaṅkaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi yāvum - kiṟistu muṉivar kālañceṉṟa piṟaku avaruṭiya cīṣarkaḷ tam ñāpakattiliruntu tiraṭṭiyaviyē. atupōlavē cākkiya muṉivaruṭiya upatēca moḻikaḷum piṟkālattil tiraṭṭappaṭṭaviyē. puttariṉ maṟi nūlkaḷip pōlavē kiṟistavariṉ maṟinūlkaḷum orē matacittāntatti vaṟpuṟuttuvatu. iraṇṭu mataṅkaḷum oru makā puruṣaṉi kaṭavuḷākak koḷkiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, putta maṟi nūlkaḷil kutama cākkiyamuṉivariviṭa avar pōtitta tarmamē muṉittirukkiṟatu; kiṟistava maṟinūlkaḷilō kiṟistuvē muṉittirukkiṟār. utāraṇamāka, putu ēṟpāṭṭil iyēcu kiṟistuviṉ cariti nāṉku ñāṉikaḷāl urikkappaṭukiṟatu. piṭakaṅkaḷil ōriṭattilum cākkiya muṉivaruṭiya cariti toṭarcciyāka urikkappaṭavilli. āṉāl pala iṭaṅkaḷil avarukkup pūrṇa ñāṉōtayam uṇṭāvataṟku muṉṉum piṉṉumuḷḷa pala ceytikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiliruntu nām kutama puttaruṭiya jīva carittiratti uṇarntukoḷḷalāmāyiṉum puttaruṭiya varalāṟṟi urippati viṭa avaruṭiya tarmōpatēcaṅkaḷi viḷakkuvatē piṭakaṅkaḷiṉ uttēcam.

tarmattiṉ koḷkikaḷi viḷakkum urikaḷukku sutta allatu cūttiraṅkaḷ eṉṟu peyar. inta cūttiraṅkaḷiṉ naṭiyippaṟṟip potuvāka oru apippirāyam kūṟalām. curuṅkaccolli viḷaṅkavittal eṉṟa mēṉmi ivaṟṟiṉ naṭiyil kāṇamuṭiyātu, itaṟku etirāka, orē aṭukkāṉa vācakaṅkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum tirumpat tirumpa varuvatu iccūttiraṅkaḷukku iyalpāyirukkiṟatu. muṉ 75-m pakkattil kūṟiyuḷḷa paṭi acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ naṭiyum ēṟakkuṟiya ippaṭiyē irukkiṉṟatu. cūttiraṅkaḷ puttaruṭiya jīvakālattil tōṉṟiya mutalurikku uṇmiyāṉa piratiyāyirukkavēṇṭumeṉṟa nōkkattuṭaṉ eḻutappaṭṭamiyē ivvita naṭikku kāraṇamātal vēṇṭum.

aṉēkacūttiraṅkaḷ puttarukkum avarit taricikka vantōrukkum ēṟpaṭṭa viṉā viṭi rūpamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. ippakutiyi airōppāvil peyarpōṉa "pḷēṭṭō"viṉ campāṣaṇikaḷukku oppākak kūṟalām. tammik kēḷvikaḷ kēṭkavantōṉuṭiya vākkiyaṅkaḷiyē perumpālum upayōkittu patil urippatum, etirviṉāvuvatum iyaṟkiyallavā? ippakutikaḷukku curuṅkaccolli viḷaṅkavittal eṉṟa cirēṣṭa kuṇam amiyāteṉiṉum ivaṟṟil colvaṉmiyum aṇiyaḻakum mikutiyāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iyaṟkiyiṉ tōṟṟaṅkaḷiliruntu tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷum upamāṉaṅkaḷum, camayōcitamāṉa mēṟkōḷkaḷum, katikaḷum, variciyāṉa niyāya vātaṅkaḷum, kēṭpōr maṉattip pala cantarppaṅkaḷil kavarak kūṭiyaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. orē tarmapōtaṉi coṟpamāṟutaluṭaṉ. tripiṭakattiṉ - vēṟoru pākattil varutalum uṇṭu.

mūṉṟu piṭakaṅkaḷāvaṉa :- 1 viṉaya piṭakam', 2 suttapiṭakam. 3 apitarma piṭakam.

' 1. viṉaya piṭakam



putta pikṣukkaḷuṭiya oḻukka muṟiyiyum nittiya niyamaṅkaḷiyum urikkum kirantaṅkaḷiṉ tokutiyām. ippiṭakattil aṭaṅkiya nūlkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu:-



* * *

nūlkaḷiṉ peyar

viṉaya piṭakattil cātāraṇamākat tarmapōtaṉikaḷ illāviṭiṉum kutamar pōtivirukṣattiṉ aṭiyil palakālam vīṟṟiruntu tavañceytu, kaṭiciyil pūrṇa ñāṉatti aṭintu, tamakkuk kiṭitta mika aritāṉa uṇmiyi ulakattukku urittu yāvariyum īṭēṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟa eṇṇaṅ koṇṭu, kāciyi nōkkic ceṉṟu, aṅkē muṉṉorukāl tamatu cīṭarāyirunta aintu tuṟavikaḷik kaṇṭu, avarkaḷukku mutaṉ mutalākat tām kaṇṭeṭutta tarmattip pōtitta vivaraṅkaḷ mahāvarkattil kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. puttar ulakattārukku mutaliṟ ceyta piracaṅkamum ip pākattil cērkkappaṭ ṭirukkiṟatu, tarma cakkarap piravarttaṉa cūttiram (mahāvarkam 6-m, at.) eṉṉum nūlil inta mutal upatēcam ceyyappaṭṭa varalāṟum ataṉ karuttum vivarikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. puttaruṭiya tarmatti naṉku uṇara avaratu mutal upatēcamākiya ‘tarma cakkarap piravarttaṉa cūttiram’ mikavum ēṟṟa ātāramām. acōkaṉum ivvitamē kūṟukiṟāṉ pōlum. avaṉ taṉatu pāpru cāsaṉattil ellōrum avaciyam maṉaṉam ceyyavēṇṭiya ēḻu cūttiraṅkaḷik kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. avaṟṟil mutalāvatākak kūṟappaṭuvatu ‘viṉaya samutkarṣam’ eṉpatu. itu, tarmacakkarap piravarttaṉa cūttirattiṉ maṟupeyarēyeṉṟu cila vitvāṉkaḷ collukiṉṟaṉar. itu, tirumpavum iraṇṭu muṟi suttapiṭakattil vevvēṟu tokutikaḷil varukiṉṟatu.

2. sutta piṭakam



sutta piṭakam allatu cūttira piṭakam, tarmatti viḷakkuvataṟkāṉa campāṣaṇikaḷum vātaṅkaḷum piracaṅkaṅkaḷum aṭaṅkiyatākum. ituvē putta maṟit tokutiyil mikavum mukkiyamāṉa pākam. itil ‘aintu nikāyaṅkaḷ' eṉṟa pirivukaḷ uṇṭu. ivvintu nikāyaṅkaḷāvaṉa. :--

i. tīrka nikāyattil aṭaṅkiyavaṟṟuḷ mikak kiyāti peṟṟa cūttiraṅkaḷic cuṭṭik kāṭṭuvōm. puttaruṭiya antiya kālattippaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷum avar cīṣarkaḷukkuc ceyta caramōpatēcamum aṭaṅkiyatu mahā parinirvāṇa cūttiram. ituvē putta maṟit tokutiyil mikavum toṉmiyuṭiyatu eṉalām.



pirammam, āṉmā, īcuvaraṉ mutaliya koḷkikaḷi ārāycci ceyyum pirahma jāla cūttiram ; ilvāḻpavariṉ kaṭamikaḷik kūṟuvatu srukāla vāta cūttiram ; tuṟavaṟattiṉ perumiyi viḷakkuvaṉa samaṇṇa pala cūttiramum, pappajja cūttiramum ; nāṉku pāvaṉikaḷik kūṟum tevijja cūttiram.

ii. majjima allatu mattiyam nikāyam tīrka nikāyattil uṭpaṭṭa cūttiraṅkaḷi viṭak kuṟukiya cūttiraṅkaḷiṉ tokutiyām. cila urikaḷ suttāntam eṉṟum vātam eṉṟum peyaruṭiyaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. acōkaṉuṭiya uḷḷam tiṭīreṉa māṟiṉataṟkuk kāraṇatti raṭṭapāla cūttiram eṉpatu viḷakkukiṟatu eṉṟu collalām. ayal nāṭukaḷik kippaṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟa aracarukkuriya pērāci maṟṟap pērācikaḷip pōlavē iḻivu eṉṟu iṅku viḷakkappaṭukiṟatu.

iii. viṣayattiṉ oṟṟumipaṟṟit tokukkappaṭṭatu samyukta nikāyam eṉpatu, orē viṣayattip paṟṟiya pala urikaḷukku allatu orē oruvaṉukkākac ceyyappaṭṭa pala upatēcaṅkaḷukku samyuktam eṉṟu peyar koṭukkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ivvitam inta nikāyattil aimpattintu samyuttaṅkaḷ uṇṭu, utāraṇamāka, inta nikāyattil nāṉkāvatu samyuttat tiṟku mārasamyuttam eṉṟu peyar. aḵtāvatu, māraṉip paṟṟik kūṟum cūttiraṅkaḷ. ivvitamē aintāvatu pikkuṇi samyuttam ; pikkuṇikaḷi aḵtāvatu peṇ - tuṟaviyarippaṟṟik kūṟuvatu.

iv. aṅkuttara nikāyam tripiṭakattuḷ mikavum virinta pākam, itil 2308 cūttiraṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. inta nikāyam patiṉoru attiyāyaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. puttarkaḷuṭiya tattuvañāṉattiṉ paṭi ellāp poruḷkaḷum viṣayaṅkaḷum ovvoru eṇkaḷāl kuṟikkappaṭum. puttarkaḷ iraṇṭuvakippaṭuvar. pirattiyēka puttarkaḷ, samyak sam puttarkaḷ. itupōlavē, mūṉṟuvakit tuṟavikaḷ, nāṉkuvaki naṉ muyaṟcikaḷ, aintu vakip pāvaṉikaḷ (allatu tiyāṉaṅkaḷ) āṟuvita vittikaḷ, uṇmi ñāṉattit tarum ēḻuvitak karuvikaḷ, nirvāṇatti aṭivataṟkāṉa eṭṭu vita paṭikaḷ, pāramiti eṉappaṭum puttaratu pattu arumperum kuṇaṅkaḷ ivvāṟu pala caṅkatikaḷum eṇkaḷikkoṇṭu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl cūttiraṅkaḷum immuṟippaṭi aṅkuttara nikāyattil aṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mutalāvatu attiyāyattil orumit taṉmiyuṭiya viṣayaṅkaḷiyum, iraṇṭāvatu attiyāyattil iraṭṭit taṉmiyuṭiyaṉavaṟṟiyum, mūṉṟāvatu attiyāyattil muttaṉmiyuṭiyaṉavaṟṟiyum, iṅkaṉamē maṟṟa attiyāyaṅkaḷil avvavvattiyāyaṅkaḷiṉ eṇṇāl kuṟippiṭappaṭum viṣayaṅkaḷiyum immaṟit tokutiyiṉ āciriyar kūṟukiṉṟār.

acōkaṉuṭiya pāṭrucāsaṉattil varum aliyava sāṉi (= āriyariṉ sittikaḷ), aṉāka tapayāṉi (= varap pōvatippaṟṟiya accam) eṉṟa iraṇṭum inta nikāyattil varum cūttiraṅkaḷēyām.

V. kuttaka nikāyam eṉpatu patiṉintu pirattiyēkamāṉa nūlkaḷ aṭaṅkiya tokuti. itil aṭaṅkiya cila nūlkaḷ mikavum pukaḻpeṟṟavi ; cila mikavum paḻamiyāṉavi. cila mika aḻakiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷeṉa matikkattak kali. āyiṉum, sakala puttarkaḷum oppukkoḷḷātaṉavum piṟkālattil tōṉṟiyaṉavumākiya nūlkaḷum ittokutiyil irukkiṉṟaṉa. itil aṭaṅkiya patiṉintu nūlkaḷiyum oṉṟupōla vivarikkavēṇṭiyatu avaciyamilli. mukkiyamāṉavaṟṟik kīḻē kuṟippiṭuvōm.

B. tammapata. innūl airōppāviluḷḷa pala pāṣikaḷilum moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. itiluḷḷa 423 nītivākkiyaṅkaḷ tiruvaḷḷuvar ceyta kuṟaṭpākkaḷippōlavum partruhariyiṉ nīticulōkaṅkaḷip pōlavum mika iṉimiyākavum aḻakākavum tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.

E. sūtta nipāta. itil 71 ciṟu cūttiraṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. itil aṭaṅkiya cūttiraṅkaḷ mikap purātaṉamāṉavi yeṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷāl karutappaṭukiṟatu, acōkaṉuṭiya pāpru cāsaṉattil collappaṭum “ upatiṣyaṉ kēḷvikaḷ" eṉṟa uri innūl 54-va tākiya cāriputtira cūttiram eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu. mōṉēya sūttiramum innūlil uḷḷatē. atu 12-vatu uri. rāhula cūttiram eṉṟa peyaruṭiya uri oṉṟum innūlil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu (23-vatu cūt). itu pāpru cāsaṉattil kuṟippiṭappaṭum upa tēcamē eṉṟu niccayamāyc colvataṟkilli. majjima nikāyattilum rāhula vātam eṉṟa oru cūttiram irukkiṟatu.

H & I. tērakāti, tērikāti. pāpru cāsaṉattil muṉikātā allatu muṉikaḷiṉ kītaṅkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa uri tērakāti eṉṟu namakkut tōṉṟukiṟatu. putta matattiṉ aṟivu vaḷaravaḷara tērakāti, tērikāti eṉṟa nūlkaḷ atikamākap pukaḻpeṟum. ēṉeṉṟāl innūlkaḷil aṭaṅkiya pāṭalkaḷil ellōruṭiya itayattiyum urukkakkūṭiya maṉōpāvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tērakāti eṉpatu tamatu vāḻ nāṭkaḷilēyē viṭutalip pataviyi aṭinta mahāṉkaḷuṭiya kītaṅkaḷām. tērikāti eṉpatu muṉkūṟiya uyar niliyi aṭinta stirīkaḷāl icikkappaṭṭa pāṭalkaḷ. ivviru pustakaṅkaḷiluḷḷa kītaṅkaḷil tuṟavaṟattil tiruptiyum, makiḻcciyum, cāntamum, amitiyum, cuṟṟattāriṭam aṉpum aṉutāpamum, uyirp pirāṇikaḷiṭam īvum, irakkamum, iyaṟki vaṉappil īṭupaṭum paricutta maṉamum mika mēṉmiyāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

J. jātaka. ikkatikaḷippaṟṟi avatārikiyil 62-m pakkattil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷōm, puttar pōti virukṣattiṉ aṭiyil pūrṇa ñāṉatti aṭintatum avarukkup pūrva jaṉmaṅkaḷil nikaḻnta campavaṅkaḷ yaravum akakkaṇṇil viḷaṅkiṉavām. avaruṭiya . tarmōpatēcaṅkaḷil avar - tammiyē tiruṣṭāntamākak kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam, tām muṉṉoru kālam puliyāyiruntatākavum, yāṉiyāyiruntatākavum, ōr ēḻi viṟakuveṭṭiyāyiruntatākavum kūṟi, ōr racamāṉa katiyic colli avar tām vaṟpuṟutta eṇṇaṅkoṇṭa nītiyi viḷakkuvār. immuṟi avaruṭiya tarma pōtaṉikku ēṟṟatāyiruntatu. piṟkālaṅkaḷil puttar tamatu pūrva jaṉma nikaḻccikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟiya katikaḷ tiraṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. jātaka eṉṟa nūl ikkatikaḷē, iṅku 449 jātakakkatikaḷ aṭaṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. "ciṅkattiṉ tōlip pōrttut tirinta kaḻuti" pōṉṟa katikaḷ pala jātakaṅkaḷil uṇṭu. ik katikaḷiṉ uriyil pala vittiyācaṅkaḷiyum nām kāṇalām.

kuttaka nikāyattil aṭaṅkiya maṟṟa nūlkaḷippaṟṟi annūlkaḷiṉ peyarukku etirākavē ciṟu kuṟippukaḷ eḻutappaṭṭiruppatik kaṇṭukoḷḷavum.

3. apitarmapiṭakam



sūtta piṭakattil piratipātikkappaṭum tarmaṅkaḷum tattuvaṅkaḷum iṉṉum virivāka iṅkuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉavēyaṉṟi putu viṣayaṅkaḷ kiṭiyā. perumpālum ip piṭakattil aṭaṅkiya nūlkaḷ piṟkālaṅkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭaṉavum oru cārāriṉ tattuva ñāṉatti māttiram viḷakkuvaṉavumāṉa kirantaṅkaḷām. ivaṟṟi iṅku vivarittuk kūṟuvatu avvaḷavu avaciyamākat tōṉṟavilli.

1. tarmasaṅkāṉi — jīvitattiṉ nilimikaḷippaṟṟi.

2. vipaṅka — campāṣaṇikaḷ.

3. katāvastup pirakaraṇa — 1000 tavaṟāṉa koḷkikaḷukku maṟuppuri.

4. putkala prakñatti — āṉmāvip paṟṟiya aṟivu.

5. tātu katā — māṉiṭa kuṇaṅkaḷiṉ vivaram.

6. yamaka — pattu iraṭṭikaḷ.

7. pras tāṉap pirakaraṇa — kāriya kāraṇaṅkaḷiṉ viḷakkam.

katāvastu eṉṟa nūl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa avacarattippaṟṟi muṉṉē 37-m pakkattil kūṟiyuḷḷōm. viṉaya piṭakattil aṭaṅkiya mūṉṟu kirantaṅkaḷum sutta piṭakattil aṭaṅkiya mutal nāṉku nikāyaṅkaḷum, kuttaka nikāyattil aṭaṅkiya patiṉintu nūlkaḷum, apitarma piṭakattil aṭaṅkiya ēḻu nūlkaḷum cērntu irupattoṉpatu kirantaṅkaḷākiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟit teṉ puttarkaḷuṭiya tirumaṟi eṉṟu kūṟutal takum. vaṭakkiluḷḷa puttarkaḷiṭattil in nūlkaḷ yāvum kiṭiyā. iṅkuk kāṇappaṭāta vēṟu pala nūlkaḷum avarkaḷiṭam irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi pala veku mēṉmiyāṉavi yeṉṟālum acōkaṉukkup piṟkālattil uṇṭāṉavi. ilaṅki, parmā mutaliya teṉ putta nāṭukaḷilum piṭakaṅkaḷil aṭaṅkiya nūlkaḷukku uriyākavum viyākkiyāṉamākavum caṅkirakamākavum maṟṟum pala nūlkaḷ pālipāṣiyil ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. mottamāka puttamata nūlkaḷ evvaḷavu eṉpatik kūṟutalum eḷitalla.

acōkaṉuṭiya cakōtaraṉāṉa mahēntiraṉum cakōtari caṅkamittiriyum cērntu pōti virukṣattiṉ kiḷiyi ciṅkaḷattiṟ koṇṭupōy nāṭṭiyati viṭa mukkiyamāṉa ceyti ulakacarittirattil illi. ituvē, taṟkālattōrukkum etirkālattōrukkum upayōkappaṭumāṟu muṟkālattiya putta camaya nūlkaḷ ēṭukaḷiṟ patintu kāppāṟṟappaṭṭataṟku ētuvāyiṟṟu, palakāraṇaṅkaḷāl intustāṉattiṉ pōti virukṣam ceḻittu vaḷaravilli. upaniṣattukaḷ eṉṟa uyar maraṅkaḷiṉ aṭarnta niḻalāṉatu avaṟṟiṉ aṭiyil tōṉṟiya pōtimarattic ceḻittu vaḷaraviṭavillipōlum, teṉ tīvil nāṭṭappeṟṟa virukṣamō, taḻittu ōṅki vaḷarntu taṉatu tāyākiya intustāṉattup pōti virukṣam kṣaṇataci aṭinta kālattil avaḷukkup pakkapalamāyiruntu, iṉṉum pala vitattil utavi, taṟkālattil taṉatu kaṉṟi ulakattila ellōrukkum niḻalkoṭuttu utavattakka virukṣamāka vaḷarac ceytirukkiṟatu. ēṉeṉṟāl, iṉṉum airōppāviluḷḷa pala tattuvañāṉikaḷ kṣaṇikavātam mutaliya paḻiya puttak koḷkikaḷip putiya vitattil urittu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. paṇṭit tamiḻar palarum puttacamayamāṉatu mēṉmiyāṉa cattiyameṉṟum, ellōrukkum potuveṉṟum nampiṉavarāvar eṉpatum nām uṇarattakkatu. atu kīḻvarum mēṟkōḷkaḷāl viḷaṅkum,

“āti mutalvaṉ aṟavāḻi āḷvōṉ

mātuyar evvam makkaḷi nīkki,

‘vilaṅkum tammuḷ verūumpaki nīkki

uṭaṅku uyir vāḻka’ eṉṟu uḷḷam kacintuka,

toṉṟu kālattu niṉṟu aṟam uritta

kuṉṟam; maruṅkil kuṟṟaṅ keṭukkum

pātapaṅkayam.”





maṇimēkali. IC-61-66.

tirumēvu patumañcēr ticimukaṉē mutalāka urumēvi avataritta uyiraṉittum uyakkoḷvāṉ, ivvulakum kīḻulakum ici ulakum iruḷ nīṅka, evvulakum toḻutētta, eḻunta ceḻum cuṭareṉṉa ilaṅkukatir ōriraṇṭum vilaṅkivalam koṇṭulava, valaṅkuciṉip pōtiyiṉ kīḻ aṟam amarnta periyōy nī.

vīracōḻiyam (maṇimēkali, pakkam, 306l varum mēṟkōḷ.)





III



itara pāṣikaḷil acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ patippukaḷ.



rāypahu tūr kurīcaṅkar ōjā; cyām suntar tās. acōka ki tarmalipiyaṉ. mūlamum hintipāṣi moḻipeyarppumuṭiya patippu. samvat 1980. vili rū. 3.

paṇṭita rāmāvatār carmā acōka piracastaya. mūlamum iṅkilīṣ camskiruta moḻipeyarppukaḷum kūṭiyatu, moratpūr, pāṭṉā , 1915.

ṭākṭar viṉseṉṭ vismit. Asoka Clarendon Press, Oxford. 3rd edition, 1920. ellā cāsaṉaṅkaḷuṭiyavum iṅkilīṣ moḻi peyarppu uṇṭu. mikutiyāka vaḻaṅkuvatu.

pūlar (G. Buhler) Asoka Inschriften. jermaṉ pāṣiyil Strassbarg 1909. cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇatti viḷakkuvatu.

E. seṉārṭ. Les Inscriptions de l'iadasi. Paris

inta miṉṉūlip paṟṟi


uṅkaḷukku immiṉṉūl, iṇiya nūlakamāṉa, vikkimūlattil iruntu kiṭittuḷḷatu[1].

inta iṇiya nūlakam taṉṉārvalarkaḷāl vaḷarukiṟatu. vikkimūlam patiya taṉṉārvalarkaḷi varavēṟkiṟatu. tāṅkaḷum vikkimūlattil iṇintu mēlum pala miṉṉūlkaḷi aṉivarum paṭikkumāṟu ceyyalām.

mikunta akkaṟiyuṭaṉ meyppu ceytālum, miṉṉūlil piḻi ētēṉum iruntāl tayakkam illāmal, vikkimūlattil immiṉṉūliṉ pēccu pakkattil terivikkalām allatu piḻikaḷi nīṅkaḷē kūṭa cari ceyyalām.

ippaṭippākkam, kaṭṭaṟṟa urimaṅkaḷōṭu (potukaḷa /kuṉu -Commons /GNU FDL )[2][3] ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, inta uriyi nīṅkaḷ maṟṟavarōṭu pakiralām; māṟṟi mēmpaṭuttalām; vaṇika nōkkattōṭum, vaṇika nōkkamiṉṟiyum payaṉpaṭuttalām
